
========== ./cmd/server/main.go ==========
package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/config"
	grpcserver "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/grpc"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/httpapi"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/kafka"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

const serviceName = "go-inventory"

func main() {
	// ─── Load config ────────────────────────────────────
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		os.Stderr.WriteString("config error: " + err.Error() + "\n")
		os.Exit(1)
	}

	// ─── Init logger ────────────────────────────────────
	log := logger.MustNew(serviceName, cfg.Env, cfg.LogLevel)
	defer func() { _ = log.Sync() }()

	log.Info("OmniTix Inventory Engine starting",
		zap.String("version", "0.1.0"),
		zap.Int("grpc_port", cfg.GRPCPort),
		zap.Int("http_port", cfg.HTTPPort),
		zap.String("redis", cfg.Redis.Addr()),
		zap.Strings("kafka_brokers", cfg.Kafka.Brokers),
		zap.Duration("default_hold_ttl", cfg.DefaultHoldTTL),
	)

	// ─── Signal handling ────────────────────────────────
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()

	sigCh := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
	signal.Notify(sigCh, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
	go func() {
		sig := <-sigCh
		log.Info("received signal, initiating shutdown", zap.String("signal", sig.String()))
		cancel()
	}()

	// ─── Connect to Redis ───────────────────────────────
	redisClient := redis.New(&cfg.Redis, log)
	defer func() {
		if err := redisClient.Close(); err != nil {
			log.Warn("error closing Redis client", zap.Error(err))
		}
	}()

	redisCtx, redisCancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 30*time.Second)
	if err := redisClient.WaitUntilReady(redisCtx, 30*time.Second); err != nil {
		log.Fatal("redis unavailable", zap.Error(err))
	}
	redisCancel()

	// ─── Load Lua scripts ───────────────────────────────
	scripts := redis.NewScripts()

	// ─── Connect to Kafka ───────────────────────────────
	kafkaProducer, err := kafka.NewProducer(&cfg.Kafka, log, "go-inventory")
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal("failed to init Kafka producer", zap.Error(err))
	}

	kafkaCtx, kafkaCancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Second)
	if err := kafkaProducer.WaitUntilReady(kafkaCtx, 10*time.Second); err != nil {
		log.Warn("kafka not ready at boot; producer will retry", zap.Error(err))
	}
	kafkaCancel()

	// ─── Init seat service ──────────────────────────────
	seatSvc := inventory.NewSeatService(
		redisClient,
		scripts,
		kafkaProducer,
		log,
		cfg.DefaultHoldTTL,
		time.Duration(cfg.MaxExtendSeconds)*time.Second,
	)

	// ─── Init GA service ────────────────────────────────
	gaSvc := inventory.NewGaService(redisClient, scripts, kafkaProducer, log, cfg.DefaultHoldTTL)

	// ─── Init best-available service ────────────────────
	baSvc := inventory.NewBestAvailableService(redisClient, scripts, seatSvc, log)

	// ─── Init expiry handler + Redis subscriber ─────────
	expirySvc := inventory.NewExpiryService(redisClient, kafkaProducer, seatSvc, gaSvc, log)

	subscriber := redis.NewSubscriber(redisClient, expirySvc, log)
	if err := subscriber.Start(); err != nil {
		log.Error("failed to start Redis keyspace subscriber — expiry events will NOT be published",
			zap.Error(err),
		)
	} else {
		log.Info("redis keyspace subscriber started")
	}

	// ─── Init gRPC handler and server ───────────────────
	handler := grpcserver.NewInventoryHandler(
		seatSvc,
		gaSvc,
		baSvc,
		redisClient,
		kafkaProducer,
		log,
	)

	grpcSrv, err := grpcserver.NewServer(cfg.GRPCPort, handler, log)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal("failed to start gRPC server", zap.Error(err))
	}

	// ─── Start HTTP gateway ─────────────────────────────
	httpSrv := httpapi.New(cfg.HTTPPort, handler, log)
	go func() {
		if err := httpSrv.Start(); err != nil && err != http.ErrServerClosed {
			log.Error("HTTP gateway error", zap.Error(err))
		}
	}()

	// ─── Smoke test hooks (Step 3.4/3.6 only — removed in 3.8) ──
	if cfg.Env == "local" && os.Getenv("INVENTORY_SMOKE_TEST") == "1" {
		runSmokeTest(ctx, seatSvc, log)
		runGaSmokeTest(ctx, seatSvc, gaSvc, log)
		runBestAvailableSmokeTest(ctx, seatSvc, baSvc, log)

		log.Info("smoke mode: waiting for kafka flush...")
		time.Sleep(1 * time.Second)

		subscriber.Stop()
		shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		_ = httpSrv.Stop(shutdownCtx)
		grpcSrv.Stop(2 * time.Second)
		kafkaProducer.Close()

		log.Info("goodbye 👋")
		return
	}

	// ─── Boot complete ──────────────────────────────────
	log.Info("boot sequence complete — waiting for shutdown signal")

	// ─── Wait for shutdown ──────────────────────────────
	<-ctx.Done()

	// ─── Graceful shutdown ──────────────────────────────
	log.Info("draining in-flight requests (10s)...")

	subscriber.Stop()

	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	_ = httpSrv.Stop(shutdownCtx)
	grpcSrv.Stop(8 * time.Second)
	kafkaProducer.Close()

	log.Info("goodbye 👋")
}

// ─── Smoke tests (unchanged from original) ──────────────────

func runSmokeTest(ctx context.Context, svc *inventory.SeatService, log *logger.Logger) {
	log.Info("=== SMOKE TEST START ===")

	occID := "smoke-occurrence-01"
	seatID := "smoke-seat-A1"
	user1 := "user-alice"
	user2 := "user-bob"

	if err := svc.CleanupSmokeTest(ctx, occID); err != nil {
		log.Warn("smoke: cleanup failed (non-fatal)", zap.Error(err))
	}

	hold, err := svc.HoldSeat(ctx, inventory.HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SeatID:       seatID,
		SeatLabel:    "Orchestra A1",
		Section:      "ORCH",
		Row:          "A",
		SeatNumber:   "1",
		UserID:       user1,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("smoke: alice hold failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("smoke: alice holds seat", zap.String("token", hold.Token))

	_, err = svc.HoldSeat(ctx, inventory.HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SeatID:       seatID,
		SeatLabel:    "Orchestra A1",
		Section:      "ORCH",
		Row:          "A",
		SeatNumber:   "1",
		UserID:       user2,
	})
	if err == nil {
		log.Error("smoke: FAIL — bob held a seat alice already holds")
		return
	}
	log.Info("smoke: bob correctly rejected", zap.Error(err))

	newTTL, err := svc.ExtendHold(ctx, inventory.ExtendHoldParams{
		Token:             hold.Token,
		UserID:            user1,
		AdditionalSeconds: 60,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("smoke: extend failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("smoke: hold extended", zap.Duration("new_ttl", newTTL))

	err = svc.ConfirmHold(ctx, inventory.ConfirmHoldParams{
		Token:   hold.Token,
		UserID:  user1,
		OrderID: "smoke-order-01",
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("smoke: confirm failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("smoke: seat confirmed (sold)")

	_, err = svc.HoldSeat(ctx, inventory.HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SeatID:       seatID,
		SeatLabel:    "Orchestra A1",
		Section:      "ORCH",
		Row:          "A",
		SeatNumber:   "1",
		UserID:       user2,
	})
	if err == nil {
		log.Error("smoke: FAIL — bob held a sold seat")
		return
	}
	log.Info("smoke: bob rejected (sold)", zap.Error(err))

	log.Info("=== SMOKE TEST PASSED ===")
}

func runGaSmokeTest(ctx context.Context, _ *inventory.SeatService, gaSvc *inventory.GaService, log *logger.Logger) {
	log.Info("=== GA SMOKE TEST START ===")

	occID := "smoke-occurrence-ga-01"
	zoneID := "smoke-zone-pit"
	zoneName := "Standing Pit"
	user1 := "user-alice"
	user2 := "user-bob"
	user3 := "user-carol"

	cleanupGa := func() {
		for _, k := range []string{
			fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:ga:%s:zone:%s:held", occID, zoneID),
			fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:ga:%s:zone:%s:sold", occID, zoneID),
			fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:ga:%s:zone:%s:capacity", occID, zoneID),
		} {
			_ = gaSvc.RawDel(ctx, k)
		}
	}
	cleanupGa()
	defer cleanupGa()

	if err := gaSvc.EnsureCapacityKey(ctx, occID, zoneID, 10); err != nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: ensure capacity failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}

	aliceHold, remaining, err := gaSvc.HoldGaZone(ctx, inventory.HoldGaParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		GaZoneID:     zoneID,
		GaZoneName:   zoneName,
		UserID:       user1,
		Quantity:     4,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: alice hold failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("ga smoke: alice holds 4", zap.Int("remaining", remaining))

	_, remaining, err = gaSvc.HoldGaZone(ctx, inventory.HoldGaParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		GaZoneID:     zoneID,
		GaZoneName:   zoneName,
		UserID:       user2,
		Quantity:     5,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: bob hold failed (unexpected)", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("ga smoke: bob holds 5", zap.Int("remaining", remaining))

	_, _, err = gaSvc.HoldGaZone(ctx, inventory.HoldGaParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		GaZoneID:     zoneID,
		GaZoneName:   zoneName,
		UserID:       user3,
		Quantity:     3,
	})
	if err == nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: FAIL — carol held 3 when only 1 available")
		return
	}
	log.Info("ga smoke: carol correctly rejected", zap.Error(err))

	capacity, held, sold, available, err := gaSvc.GetGaAvailability(ctx, occID, zoneID)
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: availability failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("ga smoke: availability",
		zap.Int("capacity", capacity),
		zap.Int("held", held),
		zap.Int("sold", sold),
		zap.Int("available", available),
	)

	if err := gaSvc.ConfirmGaZone(ctx, inventory.ConfirmGaParams{
		Token:   aliceHold.Token,
		UserID:  user1,
		OrderID: "smoke-order-ga-01",
	}); err != nil {
		log.Error("ga smoke: alice confirm failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("ga smoke: alice confirmed 4 units sold")

	capacity, held, sold, available, _ = gaSvc.GetGaAvailability(ctx, occID, zoneID)
	log.Info("ga smoke: post-confirm availability",
		zap.Int("capacity", capacity),
		zap.Int("held", held),
		zap.Int("sold", sold),
		zap.Int("available", available),
	)

	log.Info("=== GA SMOKE TEST PASSED ===")
}

func runBestAvailableSmokeTest(
	ctx context.Context,
	seatSvc *inventory.SeatService,
	baSvc *inventory.BestAvailableService,
	log *logger.Logger,
) {
	log.Info("=== BEST-AVAILABLE SMOKE TEST START ===")

	occID := "smoke-occurrence-ba-01"
	section := "ORCH"
	user1 := "user-alice"
	user2 := "user-bob"

	if err := seatSvc.CleanupSmokeTest(ctx, occID); err != nil {
		log.Warn("ba smoke: cleanup failed", zap.Error(err))
	}
	_ = seatSvc.RawDel(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:layout:%s", occID))

	layout := &inventory.LayoutCache{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		Sections: map[string][]inventory.SeatInfo{
			section: {
				{SeatID: "ba-A1", SeatLabel: "Orchestra A1", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 1, X: 100, Y: 200},
				{SeatID: "ba-A2", SeatLabel: "Orchestra A2", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 2, X: 120, Y: 200},
				{SeatID: "ba-A3", SeatLabel: "Orchestra A3", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 3, X: 140, Y: 200},
				{SeatID: "ba-A4", SeatLabel: "Orchestra A4", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 4, X: 160, Y: 200},
				{SeatID: "ba-A5", SeatLabel: "Orchestra A5", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 5, X: 180, Y: 200},
				{SeatID: "ba-B1", SeatLabel: "Orchestra B1", Section: "ORCH", Row: "B", SeatNumber: 1, X: 100, Y: 240},
				{SeatID: "ba-B2", SeatLabel: "Orchestra B2", Section: "ORCH", Row: "B", SeatNumber: 2, X: 120, Y: 240},
				{SeatID: "ba-B3", SeatLabel: "Orchestra B3", Section: "ORCH", Row: "B", SeatNumber: 3, X: 140, Y: 240},
				{SeatID: "ba-B4", SeatLabel: "Orchestra B4", Section: "ORCH", Row: "B", SeatNumber: 4, X: 160, Y: 240},
				{SeatID: "ba-B5", SeatLabel: "Orchestra B5", Section: "ORCH", Row: "B", SeatNumber: 5, X: 180, Y: 240},
			},
		},
	}
	if err := baSvc.CacheLayout(ctx, layout); err != nil {
		log.Error("ba smoke: cache layout failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}

	result, err := baSvc.FindBestAvailable(ctx, inventory.BestAvailableParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SectionCode:  section,
		Quantity:     2,
		UserID:       user1,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("ba smoke: test1 failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	log.Info("ba smoke: test1 (2 best)",
		zap.String("section", result.Section),
		zap.String("row", result.Row),
		zap.Int("count", len(result.Holds)),
	)

	if err := seatSvc.CleanupSmokeTest(ctx, occID); err != nil {
		log.Warn("ba smoke: cleanup failed", zap.Error(err))
	}

	for _, seatID := range []string{"ba-A1", "ba-A2", "ba-A4", "ba-A5"} {
		_, err := seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, inventory.HoldSeatParams{
			OccurrenceID: occID,
			SeatID:       seatID,
			SeatLabel:    "Orchestra " + seatID,
			Section:      "ORCH",
			Row:          "A",
			SeatNumber:   seatID[4:5],
			UserID:       user1,
		})
		if err != nil {
			log.Error("ba smoke: pre-hold failed", zap.String("seat", seatID), zap.Error(err))
			return
		}
	}

	result, err = baSvc.FindBestAvailable(ctx, inventory.BestAvailableParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SectionCode:  section,
		Quantity:     2,
		UserID:       user2,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Error("ba smoke: test2 failed", zap.Error(err))
		return
	}
	if result.Row != "B" {
		log.Error("ba smoke: FAIL — expected row B, got " + result.Row)
		return
	}
	log.Info("ba smoke: test2 (avoided orphan in A)",
		zap.String("row", result.Row),
		zap.Int("count", len(result.Holds)),
	)

	_ = seatSvc.CleanupSmokeTest(ctx, occID)
	_ = seatSvc.RawDel(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:layout:%s", occID))

	log.Info("=== BEST-AVAILABLE SMOKE TEST PASSED ===")
}

========== ./internal/config/config.go ==========
// Package config loads OmniTix Inventory Engine settings from environment
// variables. Local development supports a .env file; production reads
// env vars directly.
package config

import (
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/joho/godotenv"
	"github.com/kelseyhightower/envconfig"
)

// Config holds all runtime settings for the inventory service.
type Config struct {
	// ─── Environment ───────────────────────────────────────
	Env      string `envconfig:"APP_ENV" default:"local"`
	LogLevel string `envconfig:"LOG_LEVEL" default:"debug"`

	// ─── gRPC server ───────────────────────────────────────
	GRPCPort int `envconfig:"INVENTORY_GRPC_PORT" default:"50051"`

	// ─── HTTP server (health + metrics) ────────────────────
	HTTPPort int `envconfig:"INVENTORY_HTTP_PORT" default:"8080"`

	// ─── Redis ─────────────────────────────────────────────
	Redis RedisConfig

	// ─── Kafka ─────────────────────────────────────────────
	Kafka KafkaConfig

	// ─── Business logic ────────────────────────────────────
	// Default hold TTL if the request doesn't specify one.
	DefaultHoldTTL time.Duration `envconfig:"INVENTORY_HOLD_TTL_SECONDS" default:"600s"`

	// Maximum additional seconds a single Extend call can add.
	MaxExtendSeconds int `envconfig:"INVENTORY_MAX_EXTEND_SECONDS" default:"300"`

	// How many seats a single HoldBestAvailable can return.
	MaxBestAvailableQuantity int `envconfig:"INVENTORY_MAX_BEST_AVAILABLE" default:"10"`
}

// RedisConfig holds connection details for the Redis cluster.
type RedisConfig struct {
	Host     string `envconfig:"REDIS_HOST" default:"redis"`
	Port     int    `envconfig:"REDIS_PORT" default:"6379"`
	Password string `envconfig:"REDIS_PASSWORD" default:""`
	DB       int    `envconfig:"REDIS_DB" default:"0"`

	// Connection pool tuning
	PoolSize     int           `envconfig:"REDIS_POOL_SIZE" default:"50"`
	MinIdleConns int           `envconfig:"REDIS_MIN_IDLE" default:"10"`
	DialTimeout  time.Duration `envconfig:"REDIS_DIAL_TIMEOUT" default:"5s"`
	ReadTimeout  time.Duration `envconfig:"REDIS_READ_TIMEOUT" default:"3s"`
	WriteTimeout time.Duration `envconfig:"REDIS_WRITE_TIMEOUT" default:"3s"`
}

// Addr returns the "host:port" string for dialing Redis.
func (r RedisConfig) Addr() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", r.Host, r.Port)
}

// KafkaConfig holds broker and topic settings.
type KafkaConfig struct {
	Brokers     []string `envconfig:"KAFKA_BROKERS" default:"kafka:9092"`
	ClientID    string   `envconfig:"KAFKA_CLIENT_ID" default:"go-inventory"`
	GroupPrefix string   `envconfig:"KAFKA_GROUP_PREFIX" default:"omnitix"`

	// Topic prefix — matches our naming convention "omnitix.<aggregate>.<event>"
	TopicPrefix string `envconfig:"KAFKA_TOPIC_PREFIX" default:"omnitix"`
}

// Load reads config from the environment, optionally loading a .env file
// first (for local development).
//
// In production (APP_ENV=production), .env is NOT loaded — env vars are
// expected to be set by the orchestrator (Kubernetes, systemd, etc.).
func Load() (*Config, error) {
	// Try to load .env but don't fail if it doesn't exist.
	_ = godotenv.Load()

	var cfg Config
	if err := envconfig.Process("", &cfg); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("load config: %w", err)
	}

	if err := cfg.Validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("validate config: %w", err)
	}

	return &cfg, nil
}

// Validate performs semantic checks on loaded config.
func (c *Config) Validate() error {
	if c.GRPCPort <= 0 || c.GRPCPort > 65535 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid GRPCPort: %d", c.GRPCPort)
	}
	if c.HTTPPort <= 0 || c.HTTPPort > 65535 {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid HTTPPort: %d", c.HTTPPort)
	}
	if c.DefaultHoldTTL < 30*time.Second {
		return fmt.Errorf("DefaultHoldTTL too short: %s", c.DefaultHoldTTL)
	}
	if len(c.Kafka.Brokers) == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("no Kafka brokers configured")
	}
	return nil
}

// IsProduction returns true if running in a production-like environment.
func (c *Config) IsProduction() bool {
	return c.Env == "production" || c.Env == "prod"
}

========== ./internal/logger/logger.go ==========
// Package logger provides a structured logger for all OmniTix Go services.
// Wraps zap with service-specific fields and level-based output formatting.
package logger

import (
	"os"

	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"go.uber.org/zap/zapcore"
)

// Logger wraps *zap.Logger with a service name for consistent field tagging.
type Logger struct {
	*zap.Logger
}

// New creates a Logger configured for the given environment and level.
//
// In development (env=local), output is human-readable with colors.
// In production, output is JSON for log aggregators.
func New(service, env, level string) (*Logger, error) {
	zapLevel, err := zapcore.ParseLevel(level)
	if err != nil {
		zapLevel = zapcore.InfoLevel
	}

	var cfg zap.Config
	if env == "production" || env == "prod" {
		cfg = zap.NewProductionConfig()
		cfg.Encoding = "json"
	} else {
		cfg = zap.NewDevelopmentConfig()
		cfg.Encoding = "console"
		cfg.EncoderConfig.EncodeLevel = zapcore.CapitalColorLevelEncoder
	}

	cfg.Level = zap.NewAtomicLevelAt(zapLevel)
	cfg.OutputPaths = []string{"stdout"}
	cfg.ErrorOutputPaths = []string{"stderr"}

	base, err := cfg.Build(
		zap.AddCallerSkip(1),
		zap.AddStacktrace(zapcore.ErrorLevel),
	)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	base = base.With(
		zap.String("service", service),
		zap.String("env", env),
	)

	return &Logger{Logger: base}, nil
}

// MustNew panics if logger creation fails. Useful in main().
func MustNew(service, env, level string) *Logger {
	log, err := New(service, env, level)
	if err != nil {
		// Can't even log the error properly, so just print + exit.
		os.Stderr.WriteString("failed to initialize logger: " + err.Error() + "\n")
		os.Exit(1)
	}
	return log
}

// With returns a child logger with additional structured fields.
func (l *Logger) With(fields ...zap.Field) *Logger {
	return &Logger{Logger: l.Logger.With(fields...)}
}

// Named returns a child logger with a subsystem name appended.
func (l *Logger) Named(name string) *Logger {
	return &Logger{Logger: l.Logger.Named(name)}
}

// Sync flushes any buffered log entries. Call in a defer from main().
func (l *Logger) Sync() error {
	return l.Logger.Sync()
}

========== ./internal/redis/client.go ==========
// Package redis provides the Redis client pool and atomic hold primitives
// for the OmniTix inventory engine.
package redis

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/config"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// Client wraps the go-redis client with OmniTix-specific helpers.
type Client struct {
	*goredis.Client
	log *logger.Logger
}

// New creates a Redis client from config. It does NOT connect immediately —
// go-redis dials lazily. Call WaitUntilReady to verify connectivity.
func New(cfg *config.RedisConfig, log *logger.Logger) *Client {
	client := goredis.NewClient(&goredis.Options{
		Addr:         cfg.Addr(),
		Password:     cfg.Password,
		DB:           cfg.DB,
		PoolSize:     cfg.PoolSize,
		MinIdleConns: cfg.MinIdleConns,
		DialTimeout:  cfg.DialTimeout,
		ReadTimeout:  cfg.ReadTimeout,
		WriteTimeout: cfg.WriteTimeout,

		// Retry policy: 3 attempts with backoff. Redis blips shouldn't
		// kill a hold request unless they persist.
		MaxRetries:      3,
		MinRetryBackoff: 8 * time.Millisecond,
		MaxRetryBackoff: 512 * time.Millisecond,

		// Connection identity for debugging in Redis MONITOR / CLIENT LIST.
		ClientName: "omnitix-go-inventory",

		// Context timeout for pipelined commands.
		ContextTimeoutEnabled: true,
	})

	return &Client{
		Client: client,
		log:    log.Named("redis"),
	}
}

// WaitUntilReady pings Redis until it responds or the context expires.
// Used at service startup to fail fast if Redis is unreachable.
func (c *Client) WaitUntilReady(ctx context.Context, timeout time.Duration) error {
	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)

	for {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			return fmt.Errorf("redis not ready after %s", timeout)
		}

		pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 2*time.Second)
		err := c.Ping(pingCtx).Err()
		cancel()

		if err == nil {
			c.log.Info("redis is ready", zap.String("addr", c.Options().Addr))
			return nil
		}

		c.log.Debug("redis not ready yet, retrying",
			zap.Error(err),
			zap.Duration("remaining", time.Until(deadline)),
		)

		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(500 * time.Millisecond):
			// retry
		}
	}
}

// Health checks Redis connectivity. Returns nil on success.
func (c *Client) Health(ctx context.Context) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	return c.Ping(ctx).Err()
}

// Close flushes the pool and closes all connections.
func (c *Client) Close() error {
	return c.Client.Close()
}

========== ./internal/redis/keys.go ==========
package redis

import "fmt"

// Key prefixes and format strings.
//
// Naming convention: <namespace>:<scope>:<id>[:<subscope>[:<id>]]
//
// All keys are versioned implicitly through the `omnitix:` prefix — if we
// ever need a breaking change, we bump to `omnitix2:`.
const (
	prefix = "omnitix"

	// Seat hold keys.
	// Example: omnitix:seat:01H...:seat:01H...
	//          ^prefix  ^occ   ^seat
	seatHoldFmt = prefix + ":seat:%s:seat:%s"

	// Sold seat sets (one SET per occurrence).
	// Example: omnitix:seat:01H...:sold
	soldSeatsSetFmt = prefix + ":seat:%s:sold"

	// Held seat sets (one SET per occurrence, optional optimization).
	heldSeatsSetFmt = prefix + ":seat:%s:held"

	// GA zone counters.
	// Example: omnitix:ga:01H...:zone:01H...:held
	gaHeldFmt     = prefix + ":ga:%s:zone:%s:held"
	gaSoldFmt     = prefix + ":ga:%s:zone:%s:sold"
	gaCapacityFmt = prefix + ":ga:%s:zone:%s:capacity"

	// Hold token → owning key mapping.
	// Example: omnitix:token:01H...
	tokenFmt = prefix + ":token:%s"

	// Idempotency keys for repeated client requests.
	// Example: omnitix:idem:hold:abc123
	idemFmt = prefix + ":idem:%s:%s"
)

// ─── Seat keys ──────────────────────────────────────────────

// SeatHoldKey returns the Redis key for a specific seat hold.
func SeatHoldKey(occurrenceID, seatID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(seatHoldFmt, occurrenceID, seatID)
}

// SoldSeatsSetKey returns the Redis key for the set of sold seats in an occurrence.
func SoldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(soldSeatsSetFmt, occurrenceID)
}

// HeldSeatsSetKey returns the Redis key for the set of held seats in an occurrence.
func HeldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(heldSeatsSetFmt, occurrenceID)
}

// ─── GA zone keys ───────────────────────────────────────────

func GaHeldKey(occurrenceID, zoneID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(gaHeldFmt, occurrenceID, zoneID)
}

func GaSoldKey(occurrenceID, zoneID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(gaSoldFmt, occurrenceID, zoneID)
}

func GaCapacityKey(occurrenceID, zoneID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(gaCapacityFmt, occurrenceID, zoneID)
}

// ─── Token keys ─────────────────────────────────────────────

// TokenKey returns the Redis key for a hold token lookup.
func TokenKey(token string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(tokenFmt, token)
}

// ─── Idempotency keys ───────────────────────────────────────

// IdempotencyKey returns a namespaced idempotency key.
// scope: "hold", "release", "confirm", "charge"
func IdempotencyKey(scope, clientKey string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(idemFmt, scope, clientKey)
}

========== ./internal/redis/scripts.go ==========
package redis

import (
	"context"

	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
)

// Scripts holds all Lua scripts used by the inventory engine. They're loaded
// once at startup and invoked by SHA1, which is faster than sending the full
// script every time.
type Scripts struct {
	// HoldSeatAtomic holds a seat, checks the sold set, and records it in the
	// held set — all in one atomic step.
	HoldSeatAtomic *goredis.Script

	// ReleaseSeatIfOwner removes a seat hold only if the caller owns it.
	ReleaseSeatIfOwner *goredis.Script

	// ExtendSeatIfOwner extends the TTL of a seat hold only if the caller owns it.
	ExtendSeatIfOwner *goredis.Script

	// ConfirmSeatIfOwner atomically removes the hold, removes it from the held
	// set, and adds the seat to the sold set.
	ConfirmSeatIfOwner *goredis.Script

	// HoldGaZone atomically checks GA capacity and decrements.
	HoldGaZone *goredis.Script

	// ReleaseGaIfOwner decrements the held count for a GA zone by N.
	ReleaseGaIfOwner *goredis.Script

	// ConfirmGaIfOwner atomically decrements held and increments sold for GA.
	ConfirmGaIfOwner *goredis.Script
}

// NewScripts returns the canonical set of Lua scripts for the engine.
//
// All scripts follow the same conventions:
//   - Return integers: positive = success, 0 or negative = failure/special.
//   - Never use KEYS in a loop over an unbounded set (Redis Cluster safety).
//   - Receive all keys via KEYS[] and all non-key args via ARGV[].
func NewScripts() *Scripts {
	return &Scripts{
		// ─── HoldSeatAtomic ─────────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = seat hold key
		// KEYS[2] = sold seats set
		// KEYS[3] = held seats set
		// ARGV[1] = seat_id
		// ARGV[2] = serialized hold JSON
		// ARGV[3] = TTL seconds
		//
		// Returns:
		//   1 = held
		//   0 = already sold
		//  -1 = already held
		HoldSeatAtomic: goredis.NewScript(`
			if redis.call("SISMEMBER", KEYS[2], ARGV[1]) == 1 then
				return 0
			end
			local ok = redis.call("SET", KEYS[1], ARGV[2], "EX", ARGV[3], "NX")
			if not ok then
				return -1
			end
			redis.call("SADD", KEYS[3], ARGV[1])
			return 1
		`),

		// ─── ReleaseSeatIfOwner ─────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = seat hold key
		// KEYS[2] = held set
		// ARGV[1] = expected owner user_id
		//
		// Returns: 1 if released, 0 if not owned or not present.
		ReleaseSeatIfOwner: goredis.NewScript(`
			local current = redis.call("GET", KEYS[1])
			if not current then
				return 0
			end
			local data = cjson.decode(current)
			if data["user_id"] ~= ARGV[1] then
				return 0
			end
			redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
			redis.call("SREM", KEYS[2], data["seat_id"])
			return 1
		`),

		// ─── ExtendSeatIfOwner ──────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = seat hold key
		// ARGV[1] = expected owner user_id
		// ARGV[2] = additional TTL seconds
		//
		// Returns: new TTL in seconds, -1 if not owned, -2 if not present.
		ExtendSeatIfOwner: goredis.NewScript(`
			local current = redis.call("GET", KEYS[1])
			if not current then
				return -2
			end
			local data = cjson.decode(current)
			if data["user_id"] ~= ARGV[1] then
				return -1
			end
			local new_ttl = redis.call("TTL", KEYS[1]) + tonumber(ARGV[2])
			if new_ttl <= 0 then
				return -2
			end
			redis.call("EXPIRE", KEYS[1], new_ttl)
			return new_ttl
		`),

		// ─── ConfirmSeatIfOwner ─────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = seat hold key
		// KEYS[2] = sold seats set
		// KEYS[3] = held seats set
		// ARGV[1] = expected owner user_id
		//
		// Returns: 1 on success, 0 if not owned.
		ConfirmSeatIfOwner: goredis.NewScript(`
			local current = redis.call("GET", KEYS[1])
			if not current then
				return 0
			end
			local data = cjson.decode(current)
			if data["user_id"] ~= ARGV[1] then
				return 0
			end
			local seat_id = data["seat_id"]
			redis.call("DEL", KEYS[1])
			redis.call("SREM", KEYS[3], seat_id)
			redis.call("SADD", KEYS[2], seat_id)
			return 1
		`),

		// ─── HoldGaZone ─────────────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = ga held counter
		// KEYS[2] = ga sold counter
		// KEYS[3] = ga capacity
		// ARGV[1] = quantity to hold
		// ARGV[2] = TTL seconds for the held counter key
		//
		// Returns:
		//   >0  = new held count after successful hold
		//   -1  = insufficient capacity (capacity - sold - held < quantity)
		//   -2  = capacity key missing (zone not initialized)
		//
		// All reads of capacity/sold/held happen inside the script, so two
		// callers cannot both pass the capacity check against a stale view.
		HoldGaZone: goredis.NewScript(`
			local requested = tonumber(ARGV[1])
			local ttl       = tonumber(ARGV[2])

			local capacity_raw = redis.call("GET", KEYS[3])
			if not capacity_raw then
				return -2
			end
			local capacity = tonumber(capacity_raw)

			local sold = tonumber(redis.call("GET", KEYS[2]) or "0")
			local held = tonumber(redis.call("GET", KEYS[1]) or "0")

			local available = capacity - sold - held
			if available < requested then
				return -1
			end

			local new_held = redis.call("INCRBY", KEYS[1], requested)
			redis.call("EXPIRE", KEYS[1], ttl)
			return new_held
		`),

		// ─── ReleaseGaIfOwner ───────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = ga held counter
		// KEYS[2] = hold token key
		// ARGV[1] = expected user_id
		// ARGV[2] = quantity to release
		ReleaseGaIfOwner: goredis.NewScript(`
			local token = redis.call("GET", KEYS[2])
			if not token then
				return -1
			end
			local data = cjson.decode(token)
			if data["user_id"] ~= ARGV[1] then
				return -1
			end
			local qty = tonumber(ARGV[2])
			local new_held = redis.call("DECRBY", KEYS[1], qty)
			if new_held < 0 then
				redis.call("SET", KEYS[1], 0)
				new_held = 0
			end
			redis.call("DEL", KEYS[2])
			return new_held
		`),

		// ─── ConfirmGaIfOwner ───────────────────────────────────
		// KEYS[1] = ga held counter
		// KEYS[2] = ga sold counter
		// KEYS[3] = hold token key
		// ARGV[1] = expected user_id
		// ARGV[2] = quantity to move from held to sold
		ConfirmGaIfOwner: goredis.NewScript(`
			local token = redis.call("GET", KEYS[3])
			if not token then
				return 0
			end
			local data = cjson.decode(token)
			if data["user_id"] ~= ARGV[1] then
				return 0
			end
			local qty = tonumber(ARGV[2])
			redis.call("DECRBY", KEYS[1], qty)
			redis.call("INCRBY", KEYS[2], qty)
			redis.call("DEL", KEYS[3])
			return 1
		`),
	}
}

// Eval is a thin wrapper that runs a script against the client, taking
// care of the KEYS/ARGV split.
func (c *Client) Eval(ctx context.Context, script *goredis.Script, keys []string, args ...any) *goredis.Cmd {
	return script.Run(ctx, c.Client, keys, args...)
}

========== ./internal/redis/subscriber.go ==========
// Package redis — keyspace notification subscriber.
//
// Redis can be configured (see infra/redis/redis.conf) to publish events on
// `__keyevent@<db>__:expired` whenever a key expires. We use this to detect
// seat-hold and GA-hold TTLs elapsing, and to publish the corresponding
// `seat.expired` / `ga.expired` events to Kafka.
//
// # Why token keys outlive hold keys
//
// A seat hold has two Redis keys:
//
//	omnitix:seat:{occ}:seat:{seat}  ← the hold; carries the TTL
//	omnitix:token:{token}           ← the token → hold mapping
//
// If both expire at exactly the same instant, the subscriber cannot look up
// the token payload to build the expired event. To guarantee the payload is
// available, the token key is given a small TTL buffer (TokenTTLBuffer) on
// top of the hold TTL. See `seat.go` and `ga.go` for the write side.
//
// # Atomicity
//
// Redis does not guarantee event delivery. Keyspace notifications are
// fire-and-forget. A restart of the subscriber during an expiry means that
// particular expiration event is lost. This is acceptable for v1 because:
//   - The seat remains AVAILABLE in Redis regardless.
//   - The "loss" only affects viewers already connected via WebSocket, who
//     will get a fresh snapshot on their next reconnect.
//   - A reconciliation pass (Laravel `omnitix:reconcile-holds`) sweeps stale
//     orders every 2 minutes.
//
// v2 can use Redis Streams for at-least-once delivery if the audit trail
// needs to be complete.
package redis

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// TokenTTLBuffer is added to the token key's TTL on top of the hold TTL.
// This guarantees the token payload is still present when the hold key's
// expiration event arrives.
//
// Set generously: a 30s buffer costs almost nothing in memory and eliminates
// the race entirely.
const TokenTTLBuffer = 30 * time.Second

// ExpiryHandler is called when a hold key expires. The caller (typically the
// seat or GA service) is responsible for publishing the Kafka event.
type ExpiryHandler interface {
	// OnSeatExpired is called when a seat hold key expires.
	// The handler must look up the token payload from Redis (it's still there).
	OnSeatExpired(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, seatID string) error

	// OnGaExpired is called when a GA held-counter key expires.
	OnGaExpired(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, zoneID string) error
}

// Subscriber listens for Redis key expiry events and dispatches them.
type Subscriber struct {
	rdb     *Client
	log     *logger.Logger
	handler ExpiryHandler

	keyPrefix string
	db        int

	ctx    context.Context
	cancel context.CancelFunc
	wg     sync.WaitGroup

	started bool
	mu      sync.Mutex
}

// NewSubscriber constructs a keyspace-notification subscriber.
func NewSubscriber(rdb *Client, handler ExpiryHandler, log *logger.Logger) *Subscriber {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	return &Subscriber{
		rdb:       rdb,
		log:       log.Named("expiry_subscriber"),
		handler:   handler,
		keyPrefix: prefix + ":",
		db:        rdb.Options().DB,
		ctx:       ctx,
		cancel:    cancel,
	}
}

// Start begins the subscription loop. Non-blocking — the loop runs in a
// goroutine. Call Stop() to shut down cleanly.
func (s *Subscriber) Start() error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	if s.started {
		return fmt.Errorf("subscriber already started")
	}

	channel := fmt.Sprintf("__keyevent@%d__:expired", s.db)

	pubsub := s.rdb.Subscribe(s.ctx, channel)

	subscribeCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(s.ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if _, err := pubsub.Receive(subscribeCtx); err != nil {
		_ = pubsub.Close()
		return fmt.Errorf("subscribe to %s: %w", channel, err)
	}

	s.log.Info("subscribed to keyspace notifications",
		zap.String("channel", channel),
		zap.String("key_prefix", s.keyPrefix),
	)

	s.wg.Add(1)
	go s.run(pubsub)

	s.started = true
	return nil
}

// Stop gracefully shuts down the subscriber.
func (s *Subscriber) Stop() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	if !s.started {
		s.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	s.started = false
	s.mu.Unlock()

	s.cancel()
	s.wg.Wait()
	s.log.Info("subscriber stopped")
}

func (s *Subscriber) run(pubsub *goredis.PubSub) {
	defer func() { _ = pubsub.Close() }()
	defer s.wg.Done()

	ch := pubsub.Channel()

	for {
		select {
		case <-s.ctx.Done():
			s.log.Debug("subscriber context cancelled")
			return
		case msg, ok := <-ch:
			if !ok {
				s.log.Warn("pub/sub channel closed")
				return
			}
			s.dispatch(msg.Payload)
		}
	}
}

// dispatch parses an expired key and routes it to the right handler.
//
// Key shapes:
//
//	omnitix:seat:{occurrenceID}:seat:{seatID}
//	omnitix:ga:{occurrenceID}:zone:{zoneID}:held
func (s *Subscriber) dispatch(key string) {
	if !strings.HasPrefix(key, s.keyPrefix) {
		return
	}

	switch {
	case strings.HasPrefix(key, prefix+":seat:"):
		s.dispatchSeat(key)
	case strings.HasPrefix(key, prefix+":ga:"):
		s.dispatchGA(key)
	default:
		s.log.Debug("ignoring expired key", zap.String("key", key))
	}
}

func (s *Subscriber) dispatchSeat(key string) {
	parts := strings.SplitN(key, ":", 5)
	if len(parts) != 5 || parts[3] != "seat" {
		s.log.Warn("malformed seat key", zap.String("key", key))
		return
	}
	occurrenceID := parts[2]
	seatID := parts[4]

	s.log.Info("seat hold expired",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
		zap.String("seat_id", seatID),
	)

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(s.ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := s.handler.OnSeatExpired(ctx, occurrenceID, seatID); err != nil {
		s.log.Error("failed to handle seat expiry",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("seat_id", seatID),
			zap.Error(err),
		)
	}
}

func (s *Subscriber) dispatchGA(key string) {
	parts := strings.SplitN(key, ":", 6)
	if len(parts) != 6 || parts[3] != "zone" || parts[5] != "held" {
		s.log.Warn("malformed GA key", zap.String("key", key))
		return
	}
	occurrenceID := parts[2]
	zoneID := parts[4]

	s.log.Info("GA hold expired",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
		zap.String("ga_zone_id", zoneID),
	)

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(s.ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := s.handler.OnGaExpired(ctx, occurrenceID, zoneID); err != nil {
		s.log.Error("failed to handle GA expiry",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("ga_zone_id", zoneID),
			zap.Error(err),
		)
	}
}

// ─── Token payload recovery ─────────────────────────────────

// TokenPayload mirrors the JSON blob written by seat.go / ga.go into
// `omnitix:token:{token}`.
type TokenPayload struct {
	Kind         string `json:"kind"` // "seat" | "ga"
	OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
	SeatID       string `json:"seat_id,omitempty"`
	GaZoneID     string `json:"ga_zone_id,omitempty"`
	UserID       string `json:"user_id"`
	Quantity     int    `json:"quantity,omitempty"`
}

// RecoverTokenPayloadByOccurrenceSeat finds the token that refers to the
// given (occurrenceID, seatID).
//
// NOTE: this is a SCAN-based lookup and is O(tokens). For v1's concurrency
// envelope (a few thousand concurrent holds per DB) this is fine. v2 should
// maintain `omnitix:seat-token:{occ}:{seat}` as a direct reverse index.
func (c *Client) RecoverTokenPayloadByOccurrenceSeat(
	ctx context.Context,
	occurrenceID, seatID string,
) (*TokenPayload, string, error) {
	pattern := fmt.Sprintf("%s:token:*", prefix)

	iter := c.Scan(ctx, 0, pattern, 200).Iterator()
	for iter.Next(ctx) {
		tokenKey := iter.Val()

		raw, err := c.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Bytes()
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}

		var tp TokenPayload
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &tp); err != nil {
			continue
		}

		if tp.Kind == "seat" &&
			tp.OccurrenceID == occurrenceID &&
			tp.SeatID == seatID {
			return &tp, strings.TrimPrefix(tokenKey, prefix+":token:"), nil
		}
	}
	if err := iter.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("scan tokens: %w", err)
	}

	return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("no token found for seat %s in occurrence %s",
		seatID, occurrenceID)
}

// RecoverGaTokenPayload finds a token associated with a GA zone. Because
// multiple users can hold the same GA zone concurrently, this is ambiguous —
// we return the first matching token. The GA held-counter key expires only
// when there are zero held units, so this path is essentially unreachable.
func (c *Client) RecoverGaTokenPayload(
	ctx context.Context,
	occurrenceID, zoneID string,
) (*TokenPayload, string, error) {
	pattern := fmt.Sprintf("%s:token:*", prefix)

	iter := c.Scan(ctx, 0, pattern, 200).Iterator()
	for iter.Next(ctx) {
		tokenKey := iter.Val()

		raw, err := c.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Bytes()
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}

		var tp TokenPayload
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &tp); err != nil {
			continue
		}

		if tp.Kind == "ga" &&
			tp.OccurrenceID == occurrenceID &&
			tp.GaZoneID == zoneID {
			return &tp, strings.TrimPrefix(tokenKey, prefix+":token:"), nil
		}
	}
	if err := iter.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("scan tokens: %w", err)
	}

	return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("no token found for GA zone %s in occurrence %s",
		zoneID, occurrenceID)
}

========== ./internal/redis/testing.go ==========
package redis

import (
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/config"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
