// NewWithClient wraps an already-constructed go-redis client. Used by tests
// that inject a miniredis instance.
func NewWithClient(client *goredis.Client, _ *config.RedisConfig, log *logger.Logger) *Client {
	return &Client{
		Client: client,
		log:    log.Named("redis"),
	}
}

========== ./internal/redis/errors.go ==========
package redis

import goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

// Nil re-exports go-redis's sentinel error so callers outside this package
// don't need to import go-redis directly.
var Nil = goredis.Nil

========== ./internal/inventory/types.go ==========
// Package inventory implements the OmniTix atomic hold engine for seats
// and general admission zones.
package inventory

import (
	"time"
	"context"
)

// Hold represents an active seat or GA hold. It's the internal representation
// that gets serialized into Redis and returned to callers.
type Hold struct {
	Token            string    `json:"token"`
	Kind             string    `json:"kind"`               // "seat" | "ga"
	OccurrenceID     string    `json:"occurrence_id"`
	SeatID           string    `json:"seat_id,omitempty"`
	SeatLabel        string    `json:"seat_label,omitempty"`
	Section          string    `json:"section,omitempty"`
	Row              string    `json:"row,omitempty"`
	SeatNumber       string    `json:"seat_number,omitempty"`
	GaZoneID         string    `json:"ga_zone_id,omitempty"`
	GaZoneName       string    `json:"ga_zone_name,omitempty"`
	Quantity         int       `json:"quantity"`
	UserID           string    `json:"user_id"`
	ExpiresAt        time.Time `json:"expires_at"`
	CreatedAt        time.Time `json:"created_at"`
}

// SeatState mirrors the proto enum but lives natively in Go.
type SeatState string

const (
	SeatStateAvailable   SeatState = "available"
	SeatStateHeld        SeatState = "held"
	SeatStateSold        SeatState = "sold"
	SeatStateBlocked     SeatState = "blocked"
	SeatStateUnavailable SeatState = "unavailable"
)

// HoldResult is the outcome of a successful hold attempt.
type HoldResult struct {
	Hold *Hold
}

// Error codes — stable strings that map to proto Result.error_code.
const (
	ErrCodeSeatAlreadyHeld  = "SEAT_ALREADY_HELD"
	ErrCodeSeatAlreadySold  = "SEAT_ALREADY_SOLD"
	ErrCodeGaInsufficient   = "GA_INSUFFICIENT_CAPACITY"
	ErrCodeHoldNotFound     = "HOLD_NOT_FOUND"
	ErrCodeHoldNotOwner     = "HOLD_NOT_OWNER"
	ErrCodeGaCapacityMissing = "GA_CAPACITY_MISSING"
	ErrCodeInvalidRequest   = "INVALID_REQUEST"
	ErrCodeInternal         = "INTERNAL_ERROR"
)

// Sentinel errors for clean error propagation.
type Error struct {
	Code    string
	Message string
}

func (e *Error) Error() string { return e.Code + ": " + e.Message }

func NewError(code, message string) *Error {
	return &Error{Code: code, Message: message}
}

// Publisher emits inventory events to the event bus. The Kafka producer
// implements this; tests can pass a no-op or spy.
type Publisher interface {
	Publish(ctx context.Context, topicSuffix, partitionKey, correlationID string, payload any) error
}

========== ./internal/inventory/tokens.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"time"

	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"
)

// newToken returns a new ULID token. ULIDs are lexicographically sortable by
// time, which makes them great for tokens (recent tokens sort last).
func newToken() string {
	entropy := ulid.Monotonic(rand.Reader, 0)
	return ulid.MustNew(ulid.Timestamp(time.Now()), entropy).String()
}

========== ./internal/inventory/seat.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// SeatService implements atomic seat holds on top of Redis.
type SeatService struct {
	rdb       *redis.Client
	scripts   *redis.Scripts
	publisher Publisher
	log       *logger.Logger

	defaultTTL time.Duration
	maxExtend  time.Duration
}

// NewSeatService constructs the seat service.
func NewSeatService(
	rdb *redis.Client,
	scripts *redis.Scripts,
	publisher Publisher,
	log *logger.Logger,
	defaultTTL time.Duration,
	maxExtend time.Duration,
) *SeatService {
	return &SeatService{
		rdb:        rdb,
		scripts:    scripts,
		publisher:  publisher,
		log:        log.Named("seat"),
		defaultTTL: defaultTTL,
		maxExtend:  maxExtend,
	}
}

// HoldSeatParams carries the input to a HoldSeat call.
type HoldSeatParams struct {
	OccurrenceID string
	SeatID       string
	SeatLabel    string
	Section      string
	Row          string
	SeatNumber   string
	UserID       string
	TTL          time.Duration // 0 = use service default
}

// HoldSeat attempts to atomically hold a specific seat.
//
// Returns:
//   - (*Hold, nil) on success
//   - (nil, *Error{Code: SEAT_ALREADY_HELD}) if another user holds it
//   - (nil, *Error{Code: SEAT_ALREADY_SOLD}) if it's in the sold set
//   - (nil, error) on internal failure
func (s *SeatService) HoldSeat(ctx context.Context, p HoldSeatParams) (*Hold, error) {
	if p.OccurrenceID == "" || p.SeatID == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing required fields")
	}

	ttl := p.TTL
	if ttl <= 0 {
		ttl = s.defaultTTL
	}

	seatKey := redis.SeatHoldKey(p.OccurrenceID, p.SeatID)
	soldSetKey := redis.SoldSeatsSetKey(p.OccurrenceID)
	heldSetKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(p.OccurrenceID)

	token := newToken()
	now := time.Now().UTC()
	hold := &Hold{
		Token:        token,
		Kind:         "seat",
		OccurrenceID: p.OccurrenceID,
		SeatID:       p.SeatID,
		SeatLabel:    p.SeatLabel,
		Section:      p.Section,
		Row:          p.Row,
		SeatNumber:   p.SeatNumber,
		Quantity:     1,
		UserID:       p.UserID,
		ExpiresAt:    now.Add(ttl),
		CreatedAt:    now,
	}

	payload, err := json.Marshal(hold)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshal hold: %w", err)
	}

	// ─── Atomic hold: check sold, SET NX, add to held set ───
	result, err := s.scripts.HoldSeatAtomic.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{seatKey, soldSetKey, heldSetKey},
		p.SeatID, payload, int(ttl.Seconds()),
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("hold seat script: %w", err)
	}

	switch result {
	case 0:
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeSeatAlreadySold, "seat is already sold")
	case -1:
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeSeatAlreadyHeld, "seat is already held by another user")
	case 1:
		// success
	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected hold script result: %d", result)
	}

	// ─── Store the token → key mapping ──────────────────
	// Token key carries a TTL buffer so the expiry subscriber can recover
	// this payload after the hold key itself expires.
	tokenPayload, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{
		"kind":          "seat",
		"occurrence_id": p.OccurrenceID,
		"seat_id":       p.SeatID,
		"user_id":       p.UserID,
	})
	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(token)
	if err := s.rdb.Set(ctx, tokenKey, tokenPayload, ttl+redis.TokenTTLBuffer).Err(); err != nil {
		// Rollback both keys.
		_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, seatKey).Err()
		_ = s.rdb.SRem(ctx, heldSetKey, p.SeatID).Err()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("set token: %w", err)
	}

	s.log.Info("seat held",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", p.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("seat_id", p.SeatID),
		zap.String("seat_label", p.SeatLabel),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.String("token", token),
		zap.Duration("ttl", ttl),
	)

	// ─── Emit seat.held event ───────────────────────────
	if s.publisher != nil {
		_ = s.publisher.Publish(
			ctx,
			TopicSeatHeld,
			p.OccurrenceID,
			"",
			SeatHeldPayload{
				EventOccurrenceID: p.OccurrenceID,
				SeatID:            p.SeatID,
				SeatLabel:         p.SeatLabel,
				Section:           p.Section,
				Row:               p.Row,
				SeatNumber:        p.SeatNumber,
				UserID:            p.UserID,
				HoldToken:         token,
				ExpiresAt:         hold.ExpiresAt,
			},
		)
	}

	return hold, nil
}

// ReleaseHoldParams carries the input to a ReleaseHold call.
type ReleaseHoldParams struct {
	Token  string
	UserID string
}

// ReleaseHold deletes a seat hold, but only if the caller owns it.
// Idempotent: releasing a non-existent hold returns nil (no error).
func (s *SeatService) ReleaseHold(ctx context.Context, p ReleaseHoldParams) error {
	if p.Token == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or user_id")
	}

	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(p.Token)
	tokenPayload, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		s.log.Debug("release: token not found, no-op", zap.String("token", p.Token))
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("get token: %w", err)
	}

	var meta struct {
		Kind         string `json:"kind"`
		OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
		SeatID       string `json:"seat_id"`
		UserID       string `json:"user_id"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tokenPayload), &meta); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unmarshal token: %w", err)
	}

	if meta.Kind != "seat" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "token is not for a seat hold")
	}

	seatKey := redis.SeatHoldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.SeatID)
	heldSetKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(meta.OccurrenceID)

	result, err := s.scripts.ReleaseSeatIfOwner.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{seatKey, heldSetKey},
		p.UserID,
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("release script: %w", err)
	}

	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, tokenKey).Err()

	s.log.Info("seat released",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", meta.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("seat_id", meta.SeatID),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.Int("script_result", result),
	)

	return nil
}

// ExtendHoldParams carries the input to an ExtendHold call.
type ExtendHoldParams struct {
	Token             string
	UserID            string
	AdditionalSeconds int
}

// ExtendHold adds time to an existing hold, capped by maxExtend.
func (s *SeatService) ExtendHold(ctx context.Context, p ExtendHoldParams) (time.Duration, error) {
	if p.Token == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or user_id")
	}
	if p.AdditionalSeconds <= 0 {
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "additional_seconds must be positive")
	}

	maxSeconds := int(s.maxExtend.Seconds())
	if p.AdditionalSeconds > maxSeconds {
		p.AdditionalSeconds = maxSeconds
	}

	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(p.Token)
	tokenPayload, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeHoldNotFound, "hold not found or expired")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("get token: %w", err)
	}

	var meta struct {
		Kind         string `json:"kind"`
		OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
		SeatID       string `json:"seat_id"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tokenPayload), &meta); err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("unmarshal token: %w", err)
	}
	if meta.Kind != "seat" {
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "token is not for a seat hold")
	}

	seatKey := redis.SeatHoldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.SeatID)
	newTTL, err := s.scripts.ExtendSeatIfOwner.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{seatKey},
		p.UserID, p.AdditionalSeconds,
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("extend script: %w", err)
	}

	switch newTTL {
	case -1:
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeHoldNotOwner, "hold is not owned by caller")
	case -2:
		return 0, NewError(ErrCodeHoldNotFound, "hold expired")
	}

	// Extend the token key too, keeping the buffer ahead of the hold.
	tokenTTL := time.Duration(newTTL)*time.Second + redis.TokenTTLBuffer
	_ = s.rdb.Expire(ctx, tokenKey, tokenTTL).Err()

	s.log.Info("seat hold extended",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", meta.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("seat_id", meta.SeatID),
		zap.Int("additional_seconds", p.AdditionalSeconds),
		zap.Int("new_ttl_seconds", newTTL),
	)

	return time.Duration(newTTL) * time.Second, nil
}

// ConfirmHoldParams carries the input to a ConfirmHold call.
type ConfirmHoldParams struct {
	Token   string
	UserID  string
	OrderID string
}

// ConfirmHold converts a hold to SOLD. Called after payment succeeds.
// Idempotent: confirming an already-confirmed hold returns nil.
func (s *SeatService) ConfirmHold(ctx context.Context, p ConfirmHoldParams) error {
	if p.Token == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or user_id")
	}

	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(p.Token)
	tokenPayload, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		s.log.Debug("confirm: token not found, assuming already confirmed",
			zap.String("token", p.Token),
		)
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("get token: %w", err)
	}

	var meta struct {
		Kind         string `json:"kind"`
		OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
		SeatID       string `json:"seat_id"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tokenPayload), &meta); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unmarshal token: %w", err)
	}
	if meta.Kind != "seat" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "token is not for a seat hold")
	}

	seatKey := redis.SeatHoldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.SeatID)
	soldSetKey := redis.SoldSeatsSetKey(meta.OccurrenceID)
	heldSetKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(meta.OccurrenceID)

	result, err := s.scripts.ConfirmSeatIfOwner.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{seatKey, soldSetKey, heldSetKey},
		p.UserID,
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("confirm script: %w", err)
	}

	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, tokenKey).Err()

	if result != 1 {
		return NewError(ErrCodeHoldNotOwner, "hold is not owned by caller")
	}

	s.log.Info("seat confirmed (sold)",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", meta.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("seat_id", meta.SeatID),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.String("order_id", p.OrderID),
	)

	// ─── Emit seat.sold event ───────────────────────────
	if s.publisher != nil {
		_ = s.publisher.Publish(
			ctx,
			TopicSeatSold,
			meta.OccurrenceID,
			"",
			SeatSoldPayload{
				EventOccurrenceID: meta.OccurrenceID,
				SeatID:            meta.SeatID,
				SeatLabel:         "",
				OrderID:           p.OrderID,
				UserID:            p.UserID,
				SoldAt:            time.Now().UTC(),
				PriceCents:        0,
				Currency:          "USD",
			},
		)
	}

	return nil
}

// CleanupSmokeTest removes all Redis state for a given occurrence.
// Used ONLY by the smoke test — not exposed in production.
func (s *SeatService) CleanupSmokeTest(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID string) error {
	soldKey := redis.SoldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID)
	heldKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID)

	if err := s.rdb.Del(ctx, soldKey).Err(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete sold set: %w", err)
	}
	if err := s.rdb.Del(ctx, heldKey).Err(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete held set: %w", err)
	}

	// Find and delete any remaining seat hold keys.
	pattern := fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:seat:%s:seat:*", occurrenceID)
	keys, err := s.rdb.Keys(ctx, pattern).Result()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("scan seat keys: %w", err)
	}
	if len(keys) > 0 {
		if err := s.rdb.Del(ctx, keys...).Err(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("delete seat keys: %w", err)
		}
	}

	// Delete tokens pointing at this occurrence.
	// Note: KEYS pattern is acceptable here because this is a smoke-test path.
	tokenKeys, err := s.rdb.Keys(ctx, "omnitix:token:*").Result()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("scan token keys: %w", err)
	}
	for _, tk := range tokenKeys {
		val, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tk).Result()
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if strings.Contains(val, occurrenceID) {
			_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, tk).Err()
		}
	}

	s.log.Info("smoke cleanup complete", zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID))
	return nil
}

// RawDel deletes a key directly. Used only for smoke test cleanup and
// admin operations — not exposed via the public API.
func (s *SeatService) RawDel(ctx context.Context, key string) error {
	return s.rdb.Del(ctx, key).Err()
}

========== ./internal/inventory/ga.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// GaService implements atomic GA zone holds on top of Redis.
type GaService struct {
	rdb       *redis.Client
	scripts   *redis.Scripts
	publisher Publisher
	log       *logger.Logger

	defaultTTL time.Duration
}

// NewGaService constructs the GA service.
func NewGaService(
	rdb *redis.Client,
	scripts *redis.Scripts,
	publisher Publisher,
	log *logger.Logger,
	defaultTTL time.Duration,
) *GaService {
	return &GaService{
		rdb:        rdb,
		scripts:    scripts,
		publisher:  publisher,
		log:        log.Named("ga"),
		defaultTTL: defaultTTL,
	}
}

// EnsureCapacityKey sets the capacity for a GA zone if it doesn't already
// exist. Called when an event goes on sale. Idempotent.
func (s *GaService) EnsureCapacityKey(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, zoneID string, capacity int) error {
	key := redis.GaCapacityKey(occurrenceID, zoneID)

	ok, err := s.rdb.SetNX(ctx, key, capacity, 0).Result()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("set capacity: %w", err)
	}
	if !ok {
		s.log.Debug("capacity key already exists, skipping",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("zone_id", zoneID),
		)
	}
	return nil
}

// HoldGaParams carries the input to a HoldGaZone call.
type HoldGaParams struct {
	OccurrenceID string
	GaZoneID     string
	GaZoneName   string
	UserID       string
	Quantity     int
	TTL          time.Duration // 0 = use service default
}

// HoldGaZone attempts to atomically reserve N units from a GA zone.
//
// All reads of capacity/sold/held happen inside a single Lua script, so
// there is no window where two callers can both pass the capacity check
// against a stale view.
func (s *GaService) HoldGaZone(ctx context.Context, p HoldGaParams) (*Hold, int, error) {
	if p.OccurrenceID == "" || p.GaZoneID == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return nil, 0, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing required fields")
	}
	if p.Quantity <= 0 {
		return nil, 0, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "quantity must be positive")
	}

	ttl := p.TTL
	if ttl <= 0 {
		ttl = s.defaultTTL
	}

	heldKey := redis.GaHeldKey(p.OccurrenceID, p.GaZoneID)
	soldKey := redis.GaSoldKey(p.OccurrenceID, p.GaZoneID)
	capacityKey := redis.GaCapacityKey(p.OccurrenceID, p.GaZoneID)

	// ─── Atomic hold via Lua ────────────────────────────
	newHeld, err := s.scripts.HoldGaZone.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{heldKey, soldKey, capacityKey},
		p.Quantity, int(ttl.Seconds()),
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("hold ga script: %w", err)
	}

	switch newHeld {
	case -1:
		return nil, 0, NewError(ErrCodeGaInsufficient,
			fmt.Sprintf("not enough capacity for %d units", p.Quantity))
	case -2:
		return nil, 0, NewError(ErrCodeGaCapacityMissing,
			"GA zone capacity not initialized; call EnsureCapacityKey first")
	}

	// ─── Compute remaining for the response ─────────────
	// Extra GETs here are for UI feedback only, not correctness.
	capacity, _ := s.rdb.Get(ctx, capacityKey).Int()
	sold, _ := s.rdb.Get(ctx, soldKey).Int()
	remaining := capacity - sold - newHeld
	if remaining < 0 {
		remaining = 0
	}

	// ─── Build the Hold struct ──────────────────────────
	token := newToken()
	now := time.Now().UTC()
	hold := &Hold{
		Token:        token,
		Kind:         "ga",
		OccurrenceID: p.OccurrenceID,
		GaZoneID:     p.GaZoneID,
		GaZoneName:   p.GaZoneName,
		Quantity:     p.Quantity,
		UserID:       p.UserID,
		ExpiresAt:    now.Add(ttl),
		CreatedAt:    now,
	}

	// ─── Store token → zone mapping ─────────────────────
	tokenPayload, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{
		"kind":          "ga",
		"occurrence_id": p.OccurrenceID,
		"ga_zone_id":    p.GaZoneID,
		"user_id":       p.UserID,
		"quantity":      p.Quantity,
	})
	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(token)
	if err := s.rdb.Set(ctx, tokenKey, tokenPayload, ttl+redis.TokenTTLBuffer).Err(); err != nil {
		_ = s.rdb.DecrBy(ctx, heldKey, int64(p.Quantity)).Err()
		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("set token: %w", err)
	}

	s.log.Info("ga zone held",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", p.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("ga_zone_id", p.GaZoneID),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.Int("quantity", p.Quantity),
		zap.Int("new_held", newHeld),
		zap.Int("remaining", remaining),
		zap.String("token", token),
		zap.Duration("ttl", ttl),
	)

	// ─── Emit ga.held event ─────────────────────────────
	if s.publisher != nil {
		_ = s.publisher.Publish(
			ctx,
			TopicGaHeld,
			p.OccurrenceID,
			"",
			GaHeldPayload{
				EventOccurrenceID: p.OccurrenceID,
				GaZoneID:          p.GaZoneID,
				ZoneName:          p.GaZoneName,
				Quantity:          p.Quantity,
				UserID:            p.UserID,
				HoldToken:         token,
				ExpiresAt:         hold.ExpiresAt,
				RemainingCapacity: remaining,
			},
		)
	}

	return hold, remaining, nil
}

// ReleaseGaParams carries the input to a ReleaseGaZone call.
type ReleaseGaParams struct {
	Token  string
	UserID string
}

// ReleaseGaZone releases N units back to the pool.
// Idempotent: releasing a non-existent or expired hold returns nil.
func (s *GaService) ReleaseGaZone(ctx context.Context, p ReleaseGaParams) error {
	if p.Token == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or user_id")
	}

	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(p.Token)
	tokenPayload, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		s.log.Debug("release: token not found, no-op", zap.String("token", p.Token))
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("get token: %w", err)
	}

	var meta struct {
		Kind         string `json:"kind"`
		OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
		GaZoneID     string `json:"ga_zone_id"`
		Quantity     int    `json:"quantity"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tokenPayload), &meta); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unmarshal token: %w", err)
	}
	if meta.Kind != "ga" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "token is not for a GA hold")
	}

	heldKey := redis.GaHeldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.GaZoneID)
	newHeld, err := s.scripts.ReleaseGaIfOwner.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{heldKey, tokenKey},
		p.UserID, meta.Quantity,
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("release ga script: %w", err)
	}

	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, tokenKey).Err()

	s.log.Info("ga zone released",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", meta.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("ga_zone_id", meta.GaZoneID),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.Int("quantity", meta.Quantity),
		zap.Int("new_held", newHeld),
	)

	return nil
}

// ConfirmGaParams carries the input to a ConfirmGaZone call.
type ConfirmGaParams struct {
	Token   string
	UserID  string
	OrderID string
}

// ConfirmGaZone converts held units to sold. Called after payment succeeds.
// Idempotent: confirming an already-confirmed hold returns nil.
func (s *GaService) ConfirmGaZone(ctx context.Context, p ConfirmGaParams) error {
	if p.Token == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or user_id")
	}

	tokenKey := redis.TokenKey(p.Token)
	tokenPayload, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, tokenKey).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		s.log.Debug("confirm: token not found, assuming already confirmed",
			zap.String("token", p.Token),
		)
		return nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("get token: %w", err)
	}

	var meta struct {
		Kind         string `json:"kind"`
		OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
		GaZoneID     string `json:"ga_zone_id"`
		Quantity     int    `json:"quantity"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tokenPayload), &meta); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unmarshal token: %w", err)
	}
	if meta.Kind != "ga" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "token is not for a GA hold")
	}

	heldKey := redis.GaHeldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.GaZoneID)
	soldKey := redis.GaSoldKey(meta.OccurrenceID, meta.GaZoneID)

	result, err := s.scripts.ConfirmGaIfOwner.Run(
		ctx, s.rdb.Client,
		[]string{heldKey, soldKey, tokenKey},
		p.UserID, meta.Quantity,
	).Int()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("confirm ga script: %w", err)
	}

	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, tokenKey).Err()

	if result != 1 {
		return NewError(ErrCodeHoldNotOwner, "hold is not owned by caller")
	}

	s.log.Info("ga zone confirmed (sold)",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", meta.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("ga_zone_id", meta.GaZoneID),
		zap.String("user_id", p.UserID),
		zap.Int("quantity", meta.Quantity),
		zap.String("order_id", p.OrderID),
	)

	// ─── Emit ga.sold event ─────────────────────────────
	if s.publisher != nil {
		_ = s.publisher.Publish(
			ctx,
			TopicGaSold,
			meta.OccurrenceID,
			"",
			GaSoldPayload{
				EventOccurrenceID: meta.OccurrenceID,
				GaZoneID:          meta.GaZoneID,
				Quantity:          meta.Quantity,
				OrderID:           p.OrderID,
				UserID:            p.UserID,
				SoldAt:            time.Now().UTC(),
				TotalPriceCents:   0,
				Currency:          "USD",
			},
		)
	}

	return nil
}

// GetGaAvailability returns the current availability for a GA zone.
func (s *GaService) GetGaAvailability(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, zoneID string) (capacity, held, sold, available int, err error) {
	capacityKey := redis.GaCapacityKey(occurrenceID, zoneID)
	heldKey := redis.GaHeldKey(occurrenceID, zoneID)
	soldKey := redis.GaSoldKey(occurrenceID, zoneID)

	pipe := s.rdb.Pipeline()
	capCmd := pipe.Get(ctx, capacityKey)
	heldCmd := pipe.Get(ctx, heldKey)
	soldCmd := pipe.Get(ctx, soldKey)

	_, _ = pipe.Exec(ctx)

	capacity = parseIntIgnoreNil(capCmd)
	held = parseIntIgnoreNil(heldCmd)
	sold = parseIntIgnoreNil(soldCmd)
	available = capacity - held - sold
	if available < 0 {
		available = 0
	}

	return capacity, held, sold, available, nil
}

func parseIntIgnoreNil(cmd *goredis.StringCmd) int {
	v, err := cmd.Int()
	if err != nil {
		return 0
	}
	return v
}

// RawDel deletes a key directly. Used only for smoke test cleanup.
func (s *GaService) RawDel(ctx context.Context, key string) error {
	return s.rdb.Del(ctx, key).Err()
}

========== ./internal/inventory/best_available.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// SeatsByRow is a helper for grouping seats by row label.
type SeatsByRow map[string][]SeatInfo

// SeatInfo describes a seat's position for the best-available algorithm.
type SeatInfo struct {
	SeatID     string `json:"seat_id"`
	SeatLabel  string `json:"seat_label"`
	Section    string `json:"section"`
	Row        string `json:"row"`
	SeatNumber int    `json:"seat_number"` // numeric, for range math
	X          int    `json:"x"`           // map coordinate
	Y          int    `json:"y"`
}

// LayoutCache is the cached seat layout for one occurrence.
type LayoutCache struct {
	OccurrenceID string                `json:"occurrence_id"`
	Sections     map[string][]SeatInfo `json:"sections"`
	CachedAt     time.Time             `json:"cached_at"`
}

// BestAvailableService implements the contiguous-seat finder.
type BestAvailableService struct {
	rdb     *redis.Client
	scripts *redis.Scripts
	seatSvc *SeatService
	log     *logger.Logger
}

// NewBestAvailableService constructs the service.
func NewBestAvailableService(
	rdb *redis.Client,
	scripts *redis.Scripts,
	seatSvc *SeatService,
	log *logger.Logger,
) *BestAvailableService {
	return &BestAvailableService{
		rdb:     rdb,
		scripts: scripts,
		seatSvc: seatSvc,
		log:     log.Named("best_available"),
	}
}

// ─── Layout cache ───────────────────────────────────────────

// CacheLayout stores a seat layout in Redis for fast retrieval.
// Called by admin endpoints when an event goes on sale.
func (s *BestAvailableService) CacheLayout(ctx context.Context, layout *LayoutCache) error {
	if layout.OccurrenceID == "" {
		return NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing occurrence_id")
	}
	layout.CachedAt = time.Now().UTC()

	key := layoutKey(layout.OccurrenceID)
	payload, err := json.Marshal(layout)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("marshal layout: %w", err)
	}

	// 24h TTL; admin re-caches when layout changes.
	if err := s.rdb.Set(ctx, key, payload, 24*time.Hour).Err(); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("set layout: %w", err)
	}

	s.log.Info("layout cached",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", layout.OccurrenceID),
		zap.Int("sections", len(layout.Sections)),
	)
	return nil
}

// loadLayout reads the cached layout from Redis.
func (s *BestAvailableService) loadLayout(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID string) (*LayoutCache, error) {
	key := layoutKey(occurrenceID)
	raw, err := s.rdb.Get(ctx, key).Result()
	if errors.Is(err, goredis.Nil) {
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest,
			"seat layout not cached for this occurrence; call CacheLayout first")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get layout: %w", err)
	}

	var layout LayoutCache
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &layout); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unmarshal layout: %w", err)
	}
	return &layout, nil
}

// ─── Best-available core ────────────────────────────────────

// BestAvailableParams carries the input to a BestAvailable call.
type BestAvailableParams struct {
	OccurrenceID string
	SectionCode  string
	Quantity     int
	UserID       string
	TTL          time.Duration
}

// BestAvailableResult is the outcome of a successful best-available pick.
type BestAvailableResult struct {
	Holds        []*Hold
	Section      string
	Row          string
	TotalTTL     time.Duration
	RequestedQty int
	FulfilledQty int
}

// FindBestAvailable locates the best contiguous run of seats and holds them.
//
// Returns:
//   - (*BestAvailableResult, nil) on success
//   - (nil, *Error{Code: GA_INSUFFICIENT_CAPACITY}) if no run fits
//   - (nil, error) on internal failure
func (s *BestAvailableService) FindBestAvailable(
	ctx context.Context,
	p BestAvailableParams,
) (*BestAvailableResult, error) {
	if p.OccurrenceID == "" || p.UserID == "" {
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing occurrence_id or user_id")
	}
	if p.Quantity <= 0 || p.Quantity > 10 {
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "quantity must be 1-10")
	}

	// ─── 1. Load layout ─────────────────────────────────
	layout, err := s.loadLayout(ctx, p.OccurrenceID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	// ─── 2. Determine which sections to search ──────────
	sections := []string{}
	if p.SectionCode != "" {
		if _, ok := layout.Sections[p.SectionCode]; !ok {
			return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInvalidRequest,
				fmt.Sprintf("section %q not found", p.SectionCode))
		}
		sections = []string{p.SectionCode}
	} else {
		// All sections, sorted for deterministic order
		for code := range layout.Sections {
			sections = append(sections, code)
		}
		sort.Strings(sections)
	}

	// ─── 3. Fetch taken seats (sold + held) ────────────
	taken, err := s.getTakenSeats(ctx, p.OccurrenceID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("fetch taken seats: %w", err)
	}

	// ─── 4. Find candidate runs ────────────────────────
	var best *candidateRun
	for _, section := range sections {
		seats := layout.Sections[section]
		byRow := groupByRow(seats)

		// Sort rows by stage proximity. For now: lexicographic (A, B, C...).
		// Future: use layout stage position for true distance.
		rowLabels := make([]string, 0, len(byRow))
		for r := range byRow {
			rowLabels = append(rowLabels, r)
		}
		sort.Strings(rowLabels)

		for _, rowLabel := range rowLabels {
			rowSeats := byRow[rowLabel]
			sort.Slice(rowSeats, func(i, j int) bool {
				return rowSeats[i].SeatNumber < rowSeats[j].SeatNumber
			})

			runs := findContiguousRuns(rowSeats, taken, p.Quantity)
			for _, r := range runs {
				// Pick the run with the best score.
				if best == nil || r.score < best.score {
					rCopy := r
					best = &rCopy
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if best == nil {
		return nil, NewError(ErrCodeGaInsufficient,
			fmt.Sprintf("no contiguous run of %d seats found", p.Quantity))
	}

	// ─── 5. Atomically hold all seats in the run ───────
	// Use a Lua script that either holds all or none.
	holds, err := s.holdRunAtomically(ctx, p, best.seats)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &BestAvailableResult{
		Holds:        holds,
		Section:      best.section,
		Row:          best.row,
		TotalTTL:     p.TTL,
		RequestedQty: p.Quantity,
		FulfilledQty: len(holds),
	}, nil
}

// candidateRun represents a possible set of seats to hold.
type candidateRun struct {
	seats   []SeatInfo
	section string
	row     string
	score   int // lower is better
}

// groupByRow splits a slice of seats into rows.
func groupByRow(seats []SeatInfo) SeatsByRow {
	out := make(SeatsByRow)
	for _, s := range seats {
		out[s.Row] = append(out[s.Row], s)
	}
	return out
}

// findContiguousRuns returns all valid contiguous runs of length n in a row.
// A run is valid if:
//   - All its seats are available (not in the taken set)
//   - Removing it does NOT leave an orphan single seat in the row
//
// Row must already be sorted by seat_number ascending.
func findContiguousRuns(row []SeatInfo, taken map[string]bool, n int) []candidateRun {
	var out []candidateRun

	for i := 0; i+n <= len(row); i++ {
		// Check if all seats in [i, i+n) are available
		allAvailable := true
		for j := i; j < i+n; j++ {
			if taken[row[j].SeatID] {
				allAvailable = false
				break
			}
		}
		if !allAvailable {
			continue
		}

		// Check for orphan creation:
		// - Seat before the run (i-1): if it exists and is available now,
		//   after we take [i, i+n) it will still be available. But if it's
		//   an isolated single seat (i.e., i-2 is taken), it becomes orphaned.
		// - Seat after the run (i+n): same logic.
		if leavesOrphanBefore(row, taken, i) {
			continue
		}
		if leavesOrphanAfter(row, taken, i+n) {
			continue
		}

		// Score: lower = better.
		// Center-out preference: distance from the "middle" seat of the row.
		rowMid := len(row) / 2
		runMid := i + n/2
		score := abs(runMid - rowMid)

		out = append(out, candidateRun{
			seats:   row[i : i+n],
			section: row[i].Section,
			row:     row[i].Row,
			score:   score,
		})
	}
	return out
}

// leavesOrphanBefore returns true if taking a run starting at index i would
// leave a single available seat at i-1 that's trapped between taken seats
// and the run.
func leavesOrphanBefore(row []SeatInfo, taken map[string]bool, i int) bool {
	if i == 0 {
		return false
	}
	// Seat at i-1 is available currently (otherwise the run would fail earlier)
	// Check if i-2 is taken or out of bounds.
	if i-2 < 0 {
		return false
	}
	if taken[row[i-2].SeatID] {
		// Seat i-1 will be trapped → orphan
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// leavesOrphanAfter returns true if taking a run ending at index i+n would
// leave a single available seat at i+n that's trapped.
func leavesOrphanAfter(row []SeatInfo, taken map[string]bool, endIdx int) bool {
	if endIdx >= len(row) {
		return false
	}
	if endIdx+1 >= len(row) {
		return false
	}
	if taken[row[endIdx+1].SeatID] {
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// holdRunAtomically holds all seats in a run; rolls back if any fail.
func (s *BestAvailableService) holdRunAtomically(
	ctx context.Context,
	p BestAvailableParams,
	seats []SeatInfo,
) ([]*Hold, error) {
	held := make([]*Hold, 0, len(seats))

	for _, seat := range seats {
		hold, err := s.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
			OccurrenceID: p.OccurrenceID,
			SeatID:       seat.SeatID,
			SeatLabel:    seat.SeatLabel,
			Section:      seat.Section,
			Row:          seat.Row,
			SeatNumber:   strconv.Itoa(seat.SeatNumber),
			UserID:       p.UserID,
			TTL:          p.TTL,
		})
		if err != nil {
			// Roll back all previously held seats
			s.log.Warn("best-available: rollback due to hold failure",
				zap.String("failed_seat", seat.SeatID),
				zap.Error(err),
			)
			for _, h := range held {
				_ = s.seatSvc.ReleaseHold(ctx, ReleaseHoldParams{
					Token:  h.Token,
					UserID: p.UserID,
				})
			}
			return nil, NewError(ErrCodeInternal,
				"partial hold failure; rolled back. retry or pick manually")
		}
		held = append(held, hold)
	}

	s.log.Info("best-available: held run",
		zap.String("occurrence_id", p.OccurrenceID),
		zap.String("section", seats[0].Section),
		zap.String("row", seats[0].Row),
		zap.Int("count", len(held)),
	)
	return held, nil
}

// getTakenSeats returns the set of seat IDs that are either sold or held.
// Uses the maintained held-set + sold-set; no KEYS scan.
func (s *BestAvailableService) getTakenSeats(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID string) (map[string]bool, error) {
	soldKey := redis.SoldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID)
	heldKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID)

	pipe := s.rdb.Pipeline()
	soldCmd := pipe.SMembers(ctx, soldKey)
	heldCmd := pipe.SMembers(ctx, heldKey)
	if _, err := pipe.Exec(ctx); err != nil && err != goredis.Nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("fetch taken seats: %w", err)
	}

	taken := make(map[string]bool)
	if ids, err := soldCmd.Result(); err == nil {
		for _, id := range ids {
			taken[id] = true
		}
	}
	if ids, err := heldCmd.Result(); err == nil {
		for _, id := range ids {
			taken[id] = true
		}
	}
	return taken, nil
}

// layoutKey returns the Redis key for a cached layout.
func layoutKey(occurrenceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("omnitix:layout:%s", occurrenceID)
}

// abs returns |x|.
func abs(x int) int {
	if x < 0 {
		return -x
	}
	return x
}

========== ./internal/inventory/events.go ==========
package inventory

import "time"

// ─── Seat events ────────────────────────────────────────────

// SeatHeldPayload is the body of a seat.held event.
type SeatHeldPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID string    `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	SeatID            string    `json:"seat_id"`
	SeatLabel         string    `json:"seat_label"`
	Section           string    `json:"section"`
	Row               string    `json:"row"`
	SeatNumber        string    `json:"seat_number"`
	UserID            string    `json:"user_id"`
	HoldToken         string    `json:"hold_token"`
	ExpiresAt         time.Time `json:"expires_at"`
}

// SeatExpiredPayload is the body of a seat.expired event.
type SeatExpiredPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID   string `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	SeatID              string `json:"seat_id"`
	SeatLabel           string `json:"seat_label"`
	UserID              string `json:"user_id"`
	HoldToken           string `json:"hold_token"`
	HeldDurationSeconds int    `json:"held_duration_seconds,omitempty"`
}

// SeatSoldPayload is the body of a seat.sold event.
type SeatSoldPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID string    `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	SeatID            string    `json:"seat_id"`
	SeatLabel         string    `json:"seat_label"`
	OrderID           string    `json:"order_id"`
	UserID            string    `json:"user_id"`
	SoldAt            time.Time `json:"sold_at"`
	PriceCents        int64     `json:"price_cents"`
	Currency          string    `json:"currency"`
}

// ─── GA events ──────────────────────────────────────────────

// GaHeldPayload is the body of a ga.held event.
type GaHeldPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID string    `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	GaZoneID          string    `json:"ga_zone_id"`
	ZoneName          string    `json:"zone_name"`
	Quantity          int       `json:"quantity"`
	UserID            string    `json:"user_id"`
	HoldToken         string    `json:"hold_token"`
	ExpiresAt         time.Time `json:"expires_at"`
	RemainingCapacity int       `json:"remaining_capacity"`
}

// GaExpiredPayload is the body of a ga.expired event.
type GaExpiredPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID   string `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	GaZoneID            string `json:"ga_zone_id"`
	Quantity            int    `json:"quantity"`
	UserID              string `json:"user_id"`
	HoldToken           string `json:"hold_token"`
	HeldDurationSeconds int    `json:"held_duration_seconds,omitempty"`
	RemainingCapacity   int    `json:"remaining_capacity"`
}

// GaSoldPayload is the body of a ga.sold event.
type GaSoldPayload struct {
	EventOccurrenceID string    `json:"event_occurrence_id"`
	GaZoneID          string    `json:"ga_zone_id"`
	Quantity          int       `json:"quantity"`
	OrderID           string    `json:"order_id"`
	UserID            string    `json:"user_id"`
	SoldAt            time.Time `json:"sold_at"`
	TotalPriceCents   int64     `json:"total_price_cents"`
	Currency          string    `json:"currency"`
}

// ─── Topic suffix constants ─────────────────────────────────

const (
	TopicSeatHeld    = "seat.held"
	TopicSeatExpired = "seat.expired"
	TopicSeatSold    = "seat.sold"
	TopicGaHeld      = "ga.held"
	TopicGaExpired   = "ga.expired"
	TopicGaSold      = "ga.sold"
)

========== ./internal/inventory/expiry.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

// ExpiryService handles Redis key-expiry events by emitting the
// corresponding `seat.expired` / `ga.expired` Kafka events.
//
// It implements the redis.ExpiryHandler interface.
type ExpiryService struct {
	rdb       *redis.Client
	publisher Publisher
	log       *logger.Logger
	seatSvc   *SeatService
	gaSvc     *GaService
}

// NewExpiryService constructs the expiry-event handler.
func NewExpiryService(
	rdb *redis.Client,
	publisher Publisher,
	seatSvc *SeatService,
	gaSvc *GaService,
	log *logger.Logger,
) *ExpiryService {
	return &ExpiryService{
		rdb:       rdb,
		publisher: publisher,
		seatSvc:   seatSvc,
		gaSvc:     gaSvc,
		log:       log.Named("expiry"),
	}
}

// OnSeatExpired is called by the Redis subscriber when a seat-hold key's
// TTL elapses. It recovers the token payload, then publishes `seat.expired`.
func (s *ExpiryService) OnSeatExpired(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, seatID string) error {
	// Remove from the held set first — this is what makes the seat
	// "available" to the read side (GetSeatStates, getTakenSeats).
	heldSetKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(occurrenceID)
	if err := s.rdb.SRem(ctx, heldSetKey, seatID).Err(); err != nil {
		s.log.Warn("could not remove seat from held set",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("seat_id", seatID),
			zap.Error(err),
		)
	}

	// Recover the token payload. The token key outlives the hold key by
	// TokenTTLBuffer (30s), so the lookup should succeed.
	tp, token, err := s.rdb.RecoverTokenPayloadByOccurrenceSeat(ctx, occurrenceID, seatID)
	if err != nil {
		s.log.Warn("could not recover token for expired seat; publishing bare event",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("seat_id", seatID),
			zap.Error(err),
		)
		return s.publishSeatExpired(ctx, SeatExpiredPayload{
			EventOccurrenceID: occurrenceID,
			SeatID:            seatID,
			UserID:            "",
			HoldToken:         "",
		})
	}

	// Clean up the token key now that we've recovered its contents.
	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, redis.TokenKey(token)).Err()

	return s.publishSeatExpired(ctx, SeatExpiredPayload{
		EventOccurrenceID: occurrenceID,
		SeatID:            seatID,
		UserID:            tp.UserID,
		HoldToken:         token,
	})
}

// OnGaExpired is called when a GA held-counter key's TTL elapses.
func (s *ExpiryService) OnGaExpired(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, zoneID string) error {
	tp, token, err := s.rdb.RecoverGaTokenPayload(ctx, occurrenceID, zoneID)
	if err != nil {
		s.log.Debug("no token found for expired GA zone; publishing bare event",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("ga_zone_id", zoneID),
		)
		remaining := s.gaAvailable(ctx, occurrenceID, zoneID)
		return s.publishGaExpired(ctx, GaExpiredPayload{
			EventOccurrenceID: occurrenceID,
			GaZoneID:          zoneID,
			Quantity:          0,
			RemainingCapacity: remaining,
		})
	}

	_ = s.rdb.Del(ctx, redis.TokenKey(token)).Err()

	remaining := s.gaAvailable(ctx, occurrenceID, zoneID)

	return s.publishGaExpired(ctx, GaExpiredPayload{
		EventOccurrenceID: occurrenceID,
		GaZoneID:          zoneID,
		Quantity:          tp.Quantity,
		UserID:            tp.UserID,
		HoldToken:         token,
		RemainingCapacity: remaining,
	})
}

// ─── Internal ────────────────────────────────────────────────

func (s *ExpiryService) publishSeatExpired(ctx context.Context, payload SeatExpiredPayload) error {
	if s.publisher == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if err := s.publisher.Publish(
		ctx,
		TopicSeatExpired,
		payload.EventOccurrenceID,
		"",
		payload,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("publish %s: %w", TopicSeatExpired, err)
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *ExpiryService) publishGaExpired(ctx context.Context, payload GaExpiredPayload) error {
	if s.publisher == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if err := s.publisher.Publish(
		ctx,
		TopicGaExpired,
		payload.EventOccurrenceID,
		"",
		payload,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("publish %s: %w", TopicGaExpired, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// gaAvailable computes remaining capacity for a GA zone as a best-effort
// snapshot at expiry time.
func (s *ExpiryService) gaAvailable(ctx context.Context, occurrenceID, zoneID string) int {
	_, _, _, available, err := s.gaSvc.GetGaAvailability(ctx, occurrenceID, zoneID)
	if err != nil {
		s.log.Warn("could not compute GA availability at expiry",
			zap.String("occurrence_id", occurrenceID),
			zap.String("ga_zone_id", zoneID),
			zap.Error(err),
		)
		return 0
	}
	return available
}

========== ./internal/inventory/seat_test.go ==========
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/alicebob/miniredis/v2"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/config"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	omnitixredis "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	goredis "github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"
)

// ─── Test harness ────────────────────────────────────────────

type testEnv struct {
	mr      *miniredis.Miniredis
	rdb     *omnitixredis.Client
	scripts *omnitixredis.Scripts
	log     *logger.Logger
	seatSvc *SeatService
	gaSvc   *GaService
	baSvc   *BestAvailableService
	pub     *noopPublisher
}

// noopPublisher satisfies Publisher without touching Kafka.
type noopPublisher struct{ calls []string }

func (n *noopPublisher) Publish(
	_ context.Context,
	topicSuffix, _, _ string,
	_ any,
) error {
	n.calls = append(n.calls, topicSuffix)
	return nil
}

func newTestEnv(t *testing.T) *testEnv {
	t.Helper()

	mr, err := miniredis.Run()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("miniredis: %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(mr.Close)

	client := goredis.NewClient(&goredis.Options{Addr: mr.Addr()})
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = client.Close() })

	cfg := &config.RedisConfig{Host: mr.Host(), Port: mr.Port()}
	log := mustLogger(t)
	rdb := omnitixredis.NewWithClient(client, cfg, log)

	scripts := omnitixredis.NewScripts()
	pub := &noopPublisher{}

	seatSvc := NewSeatService(rdb, scripts, pub, log, 60*time.Second, 5*time.Minute)
	gaSvc := NewGaService(rdb, scripts, pub, log, 60*time.Second)
	baSvc := NewBestAvailableService(rdb, scripts, seatSvc, log)

	return &testEnv{
		mr:      mr,
		rdb:     rdb,
		scripts: scripts,
		log:     log,
		seatSvc: seatSvc,
		gaSvc:   gaSvc,
		baSvc:   baSvc,
		pub:     pub,
	}
}

func mustLogger(t *testing.T) *logger.Logger {
	t.Helper()
	return logger.MustNew("test", "local", "error")
}

// ─── Seat hold tests ─────────────────────────────────────────

func TestHoldSeat_SucceedsWhenAvailable(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	hold, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		SeatLabel:    "Orchestra A1",
		Section:      "ORCH",
		Row:          "A",
		SeatNumber:   "1",
		UserID:       "alice",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("HoldSeat: %v", err)
	}
	if hold == nil || hold.Token == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected non-empty token")
	}

	members, _ := env.rdb.SMembers(ctx, omnitixredis.HeldSeatsSetKey("occ1")).Result()
	if len(members) != 1 || members[0] != "A1" {
		t.Fatalf("expected held set to contain A1, got %v", members)
	}
}

func TestHoldSeat_RejectsWhenAlreadyHeld(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	_, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "alice",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("first hold: %v", err)
	}

	_, err = env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "bob",
	})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected second hold to fail")
	}
	invErr, ok := err.(*Error)
	if !ok || invErr.Code != ErrCodeSeatAlreadyHeld {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCodeSeatAlreadyHeld, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestHoldSeat_RejectsWhenSold(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	soldKey := omnitixredis.SoldSeatsSetKey("occ1")
	if err := env.rdb.SAdd(ctx, soldKey, "A1").Err(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("seed sold: %v", err)
	}

	_, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "alice",
	})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected hold on sold seat to fail")
	}
	invErr, ok := err.(*Error)
	if !ok || invErr.Code != ErrCodeSeatAlreadySold {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCodeSeatAlreadySold, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestHoldSeat_ExpiresAfterTTL(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	_, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "alice",
		TTL:          2 * time.Second,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("hold: %v", err)
	}

	env.mr.FastForward(3 * time.Second)

	seatKey := omnitixredis.SeatHoldKey("occ1", "A1")
	exists, _ := env.rdb.Exists(ctx, seatKey).Result()
	if exists != 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected hold key to be gone after TTL")
	}
}

func TestReleaseHold_OnlyOwnerCanRelease(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	hold, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "alice",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("hold: %v", err)
	}

	// Bob tries to release Alice's hold.
	_ = env.seatSvc.ReleaseHold(ctx, ReleaseHoldParams{
		Token:  hold.Token,
		UserID: "bob",
	})

	seatKey := omnitixredis.SeatHoldKey("occ1", "A1")
	exists, _ := env.rdb.Exists(ctx, seatKey).Result()
	if exists != 1 {
		t.Fatal("bob should NOT have released alice's hold")
	}

	// Alice releases her own hold.
	if err := env.seatSvc.ReleaseHold(ctx, ReleaseHoldParams{
		Token:  hold.Token,
		UserID: "alice",
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("alice release: %v", err)
	}
	exists, _ = env.rdb.Exists(ctx, seatKey).Result()
	if exists != 0 {
		t.Fatal("alice's own release should have deleted the hold")
	}

	members, _ := env.rdb.SMembers(ctx, omnitixredis.HeldSeatsSetKey("occ1")).Result()
	if len(members) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("held set should be empty after release, got %v", members)
	}
}

func TestConfirmHold_MovesSeatToSold(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	hold, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: "occ1",
		SeatID:       "A1",
		UserID:       "alice",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("hold: %v", err)
	}

	if err := env.seatSvc.ConfirmHold(ctx, ConfirmHoldParams{
		Token:   hold.Token,
		UserID:  "alice",
		OrderID: "order1",
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("confirm: %v", err)
	}

	sold, _ := env.rdb.SIsMember(ctx, omnitixredis.SoldSeatsSetKey("occ1"), "A1").Result()
	if !sold {
		t.Fatal("expected A1 in sold set")
	}
	held, _ := env.rdb.SIsMember(ctx, omnitixredis.HeldSeatsSetKey("occ1"), "A1").Result()
	if held {
		t.Fatal("expected A1 removed from held set")
	}
	exists, _ := env.rdb.Exists(ctx, omnitixredis.SeatHoldKey("occ1", "A1")).Result()
	if exists != 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected seat hold key gone after confirm")
	}
}

func TestBestAvailable_PicksContiguousRun(t *testing.T) {
