		t.Fatalf("expected 2 holds, got %d", len(result.Holds))
	}
}

func TestBestAvailable_RespectsOrphanRule(t *testing.T) {
	env := newTestEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	occID := "occ1"

	layout := &LayoutCache{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		Sections: map[string][]SeatInfo{
			"ORCH": {
				{SeatID: "A1", SeatLabel: "A1", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 1},
				{SeatID: "A2", SeatLabel: "A2", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 2},
				{SeatID: "A3", SeatLabel: "A3", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 3},
				{SeatID: "A4", SeatLabel: "A4", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 4},
				{SeatID: "A5", SeatLabel: "A5", Section: "ORCH", Row: "A", SeatNumber: 5},
			},
		},
	}
	if err := env.baSvc.CacheLayout(ctx, layout); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("cache layout: %v", err)
	}

	for _, sid := range []string{"A1", "A2", "A4", "A5"} {
		_, err := env.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, HoldSeatParams{
			OccurrenceID: occID,
			SeatID:       sid,
			Section:      "ORCH",
			Row:          "A",
			UserID:       "alice",
		})
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("pre-hold %s: %v", sid, err)
		}
	}

	// Bob asks for 2 → no contiguous pair exists.
	_, err := env.baSvc.FindBestAvailable(ctx, BestAvailableParams{
		OccurrenceID: occID,
		SectionCode:  "ORCH",
		Quantity:     2,
		UserID:       "bob",
	})
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected best available to fail when no contiguous pair exists")
	}
}

========== ./internal/kafka/producer.go ==========
// Package kafka wraps franz-go for producing OmniTix events.
package kafka

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/config"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/oklog/ulid/v2"
	"github.com/twmb/franz-go/pkg/kgo"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"crypto/rand"
)

// Producer emits structured events to Kafka.
type Producer struct {
	client  *kgo.Client
	prefix  string
	producerName string
	log     *logger.Logger
}

// NewProducer creates a Kafka producer. The client connects lazily on first
// produce, so this returns immediately even if Kafka is unreachable.
func NewProducer(cfg *config.KafkaConfig, log *logger.Logger, producerName string) (*Producer, error) {
	opts := []kgo.Opt{
		kgo.SeedBrokers(cfg.Brokers...),
		kgo.ClientID(cfg.ClientID),

		// Wait for all replicas to ack (single-broker dev still works).
		kgo.RequiredAcks(kgo.AllISRAcks()),

		// Idempotent producer: exactly-once semantics within a session.
		kgo.ProducerBatchCompression(kgo.GzipCompression()),

		// Small linger for batching without adding perceptible latency.
		kgo.ProducerLinger(5 * time.Millisecond),

		// Bound memory: fail if we can't produce within 100ms of batching.
		kgo.ProducerBatchMaxBytes(1_000_000), // 1 MB
		kgo.MaxBufferedRecords(10_000),
		kgo.RecordRetries(3),
		kgo.RecordDeliveryTimeout(10 * time.Second),

		// Allow auto-topic-creation (dev convenience; disable in prod).
		kgo.AllowAutoTopicCreation(),
	}

	client, err := kgo.NewClient(opts...)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("kafka client: %w", err)
	}

	return &Producer{
		client:       client,
		prefix:       cfg.TopicPrefix,
		producerName: producerName,
		log:          log.Named("kafka"),
	}, nil
}

// WaitUntilReady pings the brokers and returns when reachable.
func (p *Producer) WaitUntilReady(ctx context.Context, timeout time.Duration) error {
	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
	for {
		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
			return fmt.Errorf("kafka not ready after %s", timeout)
		}

		pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 3*time.Second)
		err := p.client.Ping(pingCtx)
		cancel()

		if err == nil {
			p.log.Info("kafka is ready")
			return nil
		}

		p.log.Debug("kafka not ready yet, retrying", zap.Error(err))

		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(500 * time.Millisecond):
		}
	}
}

// Envelope is the standard OmniTix event wrapper.
type Envelope struct {
	ID            string          `json:"id"`
	Type          string          `json:"type"`
	Version       int             `json:"version"`
	OccurredAt    string          `json:"occurred_at"`
	Producer      string          `json:"producer"`
	CorrelationID string          `json:"correlation_id,omitempty"`
	PartitionKey  string          `json:"partition_key,omitempty"`
	Payload       json.RawMessage `json:"payload"`
}

// Publish wraps a payload in an Envelope and sends it to the given topic suffix.
//
// topicSuffix: "seat.held", "ga.sold", etc. — the full topic will be
// "<prefix>.<topicSuffix>".
//
// partitionKey: the message will be routed to the partition determined by
// this key. All events with the same key go to the same partition.
func (p *Producer) Publish(
	ctx context.Context,
	topicSuffix string,
	partitionKey string,
	correlationID string,
	payload any,
) error {
	payloadBytes, err := json.Marshal(payload)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("marshal payload: %w", err)
	}

	env := Envelope{
		ID:            newEventID(),
		Type:          topicSuffix,
		Version:       1,
		OccurredAt:    time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339Nano),
		Producer:      p.producerName,
		CorrelationID: correlationID,
		PartitionKey:  partitionKey,
		Payload:       payloadBytes,
	}

	envBytes, err := json.Marshal(env)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("marshal envelope: %w", err)
	}

	fullTopic := p.prefix + "." + topicSuffix
	record := &kgo.Record{
		Topic: fullTopic,
		Key:   []byte(partitionKey),
		Value: envBytes,
	}

	// Fire-and-forget in v1: run in a goroutine, log errors. Never block
	// the caller on Kafka.
	go func() {
		// Use a background context with a timeout so a slow Kafka doesn't
		// hold resources forever.
		produceCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 15*time.Second)
		defer cancel()

		result := p.client.ProduceSync(produceCtx, record)
		if err := result.FirstErr(); err != nil {
			p.log.Error("kafka produce failed",
				zap.String("topic", fullTopic),
				zap.String("event_id", env.ID),
				zap.String("event_type", env.Type),
				zap.Error(err),
			)
			return
		}

		p.log.Debug("kafka event produced",
			zap.String("topic", fullTopic),
			zap.String("event_id", env.ID),
			zap.String("event_type", env.Type),
			zap.String("partition_key", partitionKey),
		)
	}()

	return nil
}

// Close flushes pending records and closes the client.
func (p *Producer) Close() {
	p.client.Close()
}

// newEventID returns a ULID suitable for use as a Kafka event ID.
func newEventID() string {
	entropy := ulid.Monotonic(rand.Reader, 0)
	return ulid.MustNew(ulid.Timestamp(time.Now()), entropy).String()
}

========== ./internal/grpc/convert.go ==========
// Package grpc implements the OmniTix InventoryService gRPC server.
package grpc

import (
	"time"

	commonv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/common/v1"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/inventory"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/timestamppb"
)

// ─── Result envelope ────────────────────────────────────────

func okResult() *commonv1.Result {
	return &commonv1.Result{Status: commonv1.Status_STATUS_OK}
}

func errResult(code, message string) *commonv1.Result {
	status := commonv1.Status_STATUS_ERROR
	switch code {
	case inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_INVALID_ARGUMENT
	case inventory.ErrCodeSeatAlreadyHeld, inventory.ErrCodeGaInsufficient:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_CONFLICT
	case inventory.ErrCodeSeatAlreadySold:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_CONFLICT
	case inventory.ErrCodeHoldNotFound:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_NOT_FOUND
	case inventory.ErrCodeHoldNotOwner:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_PERMISSION_DENIED
	case inventory.ErrCodeGaCapacityMissing:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_UNAVAILABLE
	case inventory.ErrCodeInternal:
		status = commonv1.Status_STATUS_ERROR
	}
	return &commonv1.Result{
		Status:       status,
		ErrorCode:    code,
		ErrorMessage: message,
	}
}

// errResultFromError extracts code/message from an inventory.Error, falling
// back to a generic INTERNAL_ERROR if the error isn't ours.
func errResultFromError(err error) *commonv1.Result {
	if err == nil {
		return okResult()
	}
	if ie, ok := err.(*inventory.Error); ok {
		return errResult(ie.Code, ie.Message)
	}
	return errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInternal, err.Error())
}

// ─── HoldToken ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func holdToToken(h *inventory.Hold) *commonv1.HoldToken {
	if h == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return &commonv1.HoldToken{
		Token:             h.Token,
		SeatId:            h.SeatID,
		GaZoneId:          h.GaZoneID,
		Quantity:          int32(h.Quantity),
		ExpiresAt:         timestamppb.New(h.ExpiresAt),
		EventOccurrenceId: h.OccurrenceID,
		UserId:            h.UserID,
	}
}

// ─── SeatState ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func seatStateToProto(s inventory.SeatState) commonv1.SeatState {
	switch s {
	case inventory.SeatStateAvailable:
		return commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_AVAILABLE
	case inventory.SeatStateHeld:
		return commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_HELD
	case inventory.SeatStateSold:
		return commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_SOLD
	case inventory.SeatStateBlocked:
		return commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_BLOCKED
	default:
		return commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_UNAVAILABLE
	}
}

// ─── RequestContext passthrough (for logging) ──────────────

// contextFromProto pulls common fields out of the RequestContext for logging.
// Returns zero values if the context is nil.
func contextFromProto(c *commonv1.RequestContext) (requestID, userID, organizerID, ip string) {
	if c == nil {
		return "", "", "", ""
	}
	return c.RequestId, c.UserId, c.OrganizerId, c.IpAddress
}

// ─── Time helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────

// durationToTTL converts a proto TTL hint (seconds) to a Go duration.
// 0 or negative returns 0, signalling "use service default".
func durationToTTL(seconds int32) time.Duration {
	if seconds <= 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return time.Duration(seconds) * time.Second
}

========== ./internal/grpc/handlers.go ==========
package grpc

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	commonv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/common/v1"
	inventoryv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/inventory/v1"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/kafka"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/redis"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"google.golang.org/protobuf/types/known/timestamppb"
)

// InventoryHandler implements the InventoryService gRPC interface.
type InventoryHandler struct {
	inventoryv1.UnimplementedInventoryServiceServer

	seatSvc *inventory.SeatService
	gaSvc   *inventory.GaService
	baSvc   *inventory.BestAvailableService
	rdb     *redis.Client
	kafka   *kafka.Producer
	log     *logger.Logger
}

// NewInventoryHandler constructs the handler.
func NewInventoryHandler(
	seatSvc *inventory.SeatService,
	gaSvc *inventory.GaService,
	baSvc *inventory.BestAvailableService,
	rdb *redis.Client,
	kafkaProducer *kafka.Producer,
	log *logger.Logger,
) *InventoryHandler {
	return &InventoryHandler{
		seatSvc: seatSvc,
		gaSvc:   gaSvc,
		baSvc:   baSvc,
		rdb:     rdb,
		kafka:   kafkaProducer,
		log:     log.Named("grpc"),
	}
}

// ─── HoldSeat ───────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) HoldSeat(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.HoldSeatRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.HoldSeatResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, ip := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "HoldSeat"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
		zap.String("ip", ip),
	)

	if req.EventOccurrenceId == "" || req.SeatId == "" {
		return &inventoryv1.HoldSeatResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing event_occurrence_id or seat_id"),
		}, nil
	}

	hold, err := h.seatSvc.HoldSeat(ctx, inventory.HoldSeatParams{
		OccurrenceID: req.EventOccurrenceId,
		SeatID:       req.SeatId,
		UserID:       userID,
		TTL:          durationToTTL(req.TtlSeconds),
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Info("hold failed", zap.Error(err))
		return &inventoryv1.HoldSeatResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	log.Info("hold succeeded", zap.String("seat_id", req.SeatId))
	return &inventoryv1.HoldSeatResponse{
		Result: okResult(),
		Token:  holdToToken(hold),
	}, nil
}

// ─── HoldGaZone ─────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) HoldGaZone(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, ip := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "HoldGaZone"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
		zap.String("ip", ip),
	)

	if req.EventOccurrenceId == "" || req.GaZoneId == "" || req.Quantity <= 0 {
		return &inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing required fields"),
		}, nil
	}

	hold, remaining, err := h.gaSvc.HoldGaZone(ctx, inventory.HoldGaParams{
		OccurrenceID: req.EventOccurrenceId,
		GaZoneID:     req.GaZoneId,
		UserID:       userID,
		Quantity:     int(req.Quantity),
		TTL:          durationToTTL(req.TtlSeconds),
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Info("ga hold failed", zap.Error(err))
		return &inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	log.Info("ga hold succeeded",
		zap.String("ga_zone_id", req.GaZoneId),
		zap.Int("quantity", int(req.Quantity)),
	)
	return &inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneResponse{
		Result:            okResult(),
		Token:             holdToToken(hold),
		RemainingCapacity: int32(remaining),
	}, nil
}

// ─── HoldBestAvailable ──────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) HoldBestAvailable(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.HoldBestAvailableRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.HoldBestAvailableResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, ip := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "HoldBestAvailable"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
		zap.String("ip", ip),
	)

	if req.EventOccurrenceId == "" || req.Quantity <= 0 {
		return &inventoryv1.HoldBestAvailableResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing required fields"),
		}, nil
	}

	result, err := h.baSvc.FindBestAvailable(ctx, inventory.BestAvailableParams{
		OccurrenceID: req.EventOccurrenceId,
		SectionCode:  req.SectionCode,
		Quantity:     int(req.Quantity),
		UserID:       userID,
		TTL:          durationToTTL(req.TtlSeconds),
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Info("best available failed", zap.Error(err))
		return &inventoryv1.HoldBestAvailableResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	tokens := make([]*commonv1.HoldToken, 0, len(result.Holds))
	for _, h := range result.Holds {
		tokens = append(tokens, holdToToken(h))
	}

	log.Info("best available succeeded",
		zap.String("section", result.Section),
		zap.String("row", result.Row),
		zap.Int("count", len(result.Holds)),
	)

	return &inventoryv1.HoldBestAvailableResponse{
		Result:          okResult(),
		Tokens:          tokens,
		SelectedSection: result.Section,
		SelectedRow:     result.Row,
	}, nil
}

// ─── ReleaseHold ────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) ReleaseHold(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, _ := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "ReleaseHold"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
	)

	if req.Token == "" {
		return &inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token"),
		}, nil
	}

	// Try seat release first; if the token isn't a seat, try GA.
	err := h.seatSvc.ReleaseHold(ctx, inventory.ReleaseHoldParams{
		Token:  req.Token,
		UserID: userID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		if err2 := h.gaSvc.ReleaseGaZone(ctx, inventory.ReleaseGaParams{
			Token:  req.Token,
			UserID: userID,
		}); err2 != nil {
			log.Info("release failed", zap.Error(err2))
			return &inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldResponse{
				Result: errResultFromError(err2),
			}, nil
		}
	}

	return &inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldResponse{Result: okResult()}, nil
}

// ─── ExtendHold ─────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) ExtendHold(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.ExtendHoldRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.ExtendHoldResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, _ := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "ExtendHold"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
	)

	if req.Token == "" || req.AdditionalSeconds <= 0 {
		return &inventoryv1.ExtendHoldResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token or additional_seconds"),
		}, nil
	}

	newTTL, err := h.seatSvc.ExtendHold(ctx, inventory.ExtendHoldParams{
		Token:             req.Token,
		UserID:            userID,
		AdditionalSeconds: int(req.AdditionalSeconds),
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Info("extend failed", zap.Error(err))
		return &inventoryv1.ExtendHoldResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	return &inventoryv1.ExtendHoldResponse{
		Result:         okResult(),
		NewExpiresAt:   timestamppb.New(time.Now().Add(newTTL)),
		ExtensionsUsed: 1,
	}, nil
}

// ─── ConfirmHold ────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) ConfirmHold(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.ConfirmHoldRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.ConfirmHoldResponse, error) {
	reqID, userID, _, _ := contextFromProto(req.Context)
	log := h.log.With(
		zap.String("rpc", "ConfirmHold"),
		zap.String("request_id", reqID),
		zap.String("order_id", req.OrderId),
	)

	if req.Token == "" {
		return &inventoryv1.ConfirmHoldResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing token"),
		}, nil
	}

	// Try seat confirm first; if the token isn't a seat, try GA.
	err := h.seatSvc.ConfirmHold(ctx, inventory.ConfirmHoldParams{
		Token:   req.Token,
		UserID:  userID,
		OrderID: req.OrderId,
	})
	if err != nil {
		if err2 := h.gaSvc.ConfirmGaZone(ctx, inventory.ConfirmGaParams{
			Token:   req.Token,
			UserID:  userID,
			OrderID: req.OrderId,
		}); err2 != nil {
			log.Info("confirm failed", zap.Error(err2))
			return &inventoryv1.ConfirmHoldResponse{
				Result: errResultFromError(err2),
			}, nil
		}
	}

	return &inventoryv1.ConfirmHoldResponse{Result: okResult()}, nil
}

// ─── GetSeatStates ──────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) GetSeatStates(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesResponse, error) {
	if req.EventOccurrenceId == "" {
		return &inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing event_occurrence_id"),
		}, nil
	}

	soldKey := redis.SoldSeatsSetKey(req.EventOccurrenceId)
	heldKey := redis.HeldSeatsSetKey(req.EventOccurrenceId)

	// Pipeline both SMEMBERS calls; ignore redis.Nil errors (empty sets
	// return redis.Nil in some client configurations).
	pipe := h.rdb.Pipeline()
	soldCmd := pipe.SMembers(ctx, soldKey)
	heldCmd := pipe.SMembers(ctx, heldKey)
	if _, err := pipe.Exec(ctx); err != nil && err != redis.Nil {
		return &inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	soldIDs, _ := soldCmd.Result()
	heldIDs, _ := heldCmd.Result()

	soldSet := make(map[string]bool, len(soldIDs))
	for _, id := range soldIDs {
		soldSet[id] = true
	}
	heldSet := make(map[string]bool, len(heldIDs))
	for _, id := range heldIDs {
		heldSet[id] = true
	}

	// Build the response. If req.SeatIds is empty, we return all seats
	// we know the state of (sold + held). Otherwise filter to the requested IDs.
	requested := req.SeatIds
	if len(requested) == 0 {
		for id := range soldSet {
			requested = append(requested, id)
		}
		for id := range heldSet {
			requested = append(requested, id)
		}
	}

	entries := make([]*inventoryv1.SeatStateEntry, 0, len(requested))
	for _, id := range requested {
		state := inventory.SeatStateAvailable
		if soldSet[id] {
			state = inventory.SeatStateSold
		} else if heldSet[id] {
			state = inventory.SeatStateHeld
		}
		entries = append(entries, &inventoryv1.SeatStateEntry{
			SeatId: id,
			State:  seatStateToProto(state),
		})
	}

	return &inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesResponse{
		Result: okResult(),
		Seats:  entries,
	}, nil
}

// ─── GetGaAvailability ──────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) GetGaAvailability(
	ctx context.Context,
	req *inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityResponse, error) {
	if req.EventOccurrenceId == "" || req.GaZoneId == "" {
		return &inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityResponse{
			Result: errResult(inventory.ErrCodeInvalidRequest, "missing required fields"),
		}, nil
	}

	capacity, held, sold, available, err := h.gaSvc.GetGaAvailability(
		ctx, req.EventOccurrenceId, req.GaZoneId,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return &inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityResponse{
			Result: errResultFromError(err),
		}, nil
	}

	return &inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityResponse{
		Result:        okResult(),
		TotalCapacity: int32(capacity),
		Held:          int32(held),
		Sold:          int32(sold),
		Available:     int32(available),
	}, nil
}

// ─── Health ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *InventoryHandler) Health(
	ctx context.Context,
	_ *inventoryv1.HealthRequest,
) (*inventoryv1.HealthResponse, error) {
	redisOK := h.rdb.Health(ctx) == nil
	kafkaOK := true // producer check would require a Ping; skip for now

	return &inventoryv1.HealthResponse{
		Healthy:        redisOK && kafkaOK,
		Version:        "0.1.0",
		RedisConnected: redisOK,
		KafkaConnected: kafkaOK,
		CheckedAt:      timestamppb.Now(),
	}, nil
}

========== ./internal/grpc/server.go ==========
package grpc

import (
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"time"

	inventoryv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/inventory/v1"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/reflection"
)

// Server wraps the gRPC server lifecycle.
type Server struct {
	grpc *grpc.Server
	lis  net.Listener
	log  *logger.Logger
}

// NewServer creates and starts a gRPC server on the given port.
func NewServer(port int, handler *InventoryHandler, log *logger.Logger) (*Server, error) {
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", fmt.Sprintf(":%d", port))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("listen on :%d: %w", port, err)
	}

	grpcServer := grpc.NewServer(
		grpc.MaxRecvMsgSize(4*1024*1024),  // 4MB
		grpc.MaxSendMsgSize(4*1024*1024),  // 4MB
	)

	// Register our service
	inventoryv1.RegisterInventoryServiceServer(grpcServer, handler)

	// Enable server reflection — lets grpcurl discover methods without .proto files
	reflection.Register(grpcServer)

	srv := &Server{
		grpc: grpcServer,
		lis:  lis,
		log:  log.Named("grpc_server"),
	}

	// Start serving in a goroutine
	go func() {
		srv.log.Info("gRPC server listening",
			zap.Int("port", port),
			zap.String("service", "InventoryService"),
		)
		if err := grpcServer.Serve(lis); err != nil {
			srv.log.Error("gRPC server stopped", zap.Error(err))
		}
	}()

	return srv, nil
}

// Stop gracefully shuts down the gRPC server, waiting for in-flight RPCs
// up to the given timeout.
func (s *Server) Stop(timeout time.Duration) {
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() {
		s.grpc.GracefulStop()
		close(done)
	}()

	select {
	case <-done:
		s.log.Info("gRPC server stopped gracefully")
	case <-time.After(timeout):
		s.log.Warn("gRPC graceful stop timed out, forcing")
		s.grpc.Stop()
	}
}

========== ./internal/httpapi/server.go ==========
// Package httpapi exposes a JSON gateway in front of the gRPC InventoryService.
// Used by Laravel and other clients that can't easily speak gRPC.
package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	commonv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/common/v1"
	inventoryv1 "github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/gen/go/omnitix/inventory/v1"
	"github.com/omnitix/go-inventory/internal/logger"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

type Server struct {
	handler inventoryv1.InventoryServiceServer
	log     *logger.Logger
	srv     *http.Server
}

func New(port int, handler inventoryv1.InventoryServiceServer, log *logger.Logger) *Server {
	s := &Server{handler: handler, log: log.Named("http")}

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health", s.handleHealth)
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /api/v1/seats/hold", s.handleHoldSeat)
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /api/v1/seats/hold-ga", s.handleHoldGaZone)
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /api/v1/seats/release", s.handleReleaseHold)
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /api/v1/seats/confirm", s.handleConfirmHold)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/seats/states", s.handleGetSeatStates)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/ga/availability", s.handleGetGaAvailability)

	s.srv = &http.Server{
		Addr:              fmt.Sprintf(":%d", port),
		Handler:           mux,
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
	}

	return s
}

func (s *Server) Start() error {
	s.log.Info("HTTP gateway listening", zap.String("addr", s.srv.Addr))
	return s.srv.ListenAndServe()
}

func (s *Server) Stop(ctx context.Context) error {
	return s.srv.Shutdown(ctx)
}

// ─── Handlers ───────────────────────────────────────────────

func (s *Server) handleHealth(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"status":  "ok",
		"service": "go-inventory",
	})
}

type holdSeatRequest struct {
	OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
	SeatID       string `json:"seat_id"`
	UserID       string `json:"user_id"`
	TTLSeconds   int32  `json:"ttl_seconds"`
}

func (s *Server) handleHoldSeat(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req holdSeatRequest
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_json", err.Error())
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	resp, err := s.handler.HoldSeat(ctx, &inventoryv1.HoldSeatRequest{
		Context:           &commonv1.RequestContext{UserId: req.UserID},
		EventOccurrenceId: req.OccurrenceID,
		SeatId:            req.SeatID,
		TtlSeconds:        req.TTLSeconds,
	})
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "grpc_error", err.Error())
		return
	}

	if resp.Result.Status != commonv1.Status_STATUS_OK {
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, resp.Result.ErrorCode, resp.Result.ErrorMessage)
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"data": map[string]any{
			"token":      resp.Token.Token,
			"seat_id":    resp.Token.SeatId,
			"expires_at": resp.Token.ExpiresAt.AsTime().Format(time.RFC3339),
			"user_id":    resp.Token.UserId,
		},
	})
}

type holdGaRequest struct {
	OccurrenceID string `json:"occurrence_id"`
	GaZoneID     string `json:"ga_zone_id"`
	UserID       string `json:"user_id"`
	Quantity     int32  `json:"quantity"`
	TTLSeconds   int32  `json:"ttl_seconds"`
}

func (s *Server) handleHoldGaZone(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req holdGaRequest
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_json", err.Error())
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	resp, err := s.handler.HoldGaZone(ctx, &inventoryv1.HoldGaZoneRequest{
		Context:           &commonv1.RequestContext{UserId: req.UserID},
		EventOccurrenceId: req.OccurrenceID,
		GaZoneId:          req.GaZoneID,
		Quantity:          req.Quantity,
		TtlSeconds:        req.TTLSeconds,
	})
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "grpc_error", err.Error())
		return
	}
	if resp.Result.Status != commonv1.Status_STATUS_OK {
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, resp.Result.ErrorCode, resp.Result.ErrorMessage)
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"data": map[string]any{
			"token":              resp.Token.Token,
			"ga_zone_id":         resp.Token.GaZoneId,
			"quantity":           resp.Token.Quantity,
			"expires_at":         resp.Token.ExpiresAt.AsTime().Format(time.RFC3339),
			"remaining_capacity": resp.RemainingCapacity,
		},
	})
}

func (s *Server) handleReleaseHold(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Token  string `json:"token"`
		UserID string `json:"user_id"`
	}
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_json", err.Error())
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	resp, err := s.handler.ReleaseHold(ctx, &inventoryv1.ReleaseHoldRequest{
		Context: &commonv1.RequestContext{UserId: req.UserID},
		Token:   req.Token,
	})
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "grpc_error", err.Error())
		return
	}
	if resp.Result.Status != commonv1.Status_STATUS_OK {
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, resp.Result.ErrorCode, resp.Result.ErrorMessage)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"data": map[string]any{}})
}

func (s *Server) handleConfirmHold(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {

}

func (s *Server) handleGetSeatStates(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	occurrenceID := r.URL.Query().Get("occurrence_id")
	if occurrenceID == "" {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "missing_occurrence_id", "")
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	resp, err := s.handler.GetSeatStates(ctx, &inventoryv1.GetSeatStatesRequest{
		EventOccurrenceId: occurrenceID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "grpc_error", err.Error())
		return
	}

	// Split into "sold" and "held" arrays for the frontend.
	// Initialize to empty slices so JSON marshaling produces [] instead of null.
	sold := []string{}
	held := []string{}
	for _, e := range resp.Seats {
		switch e.State {
		case commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_SOLD:
			sold = append(sold, e.SeatId)
		case commonv1.SeatState_SEAT_STATE_HELD:
			held = append(held, e.SeatId)
		}
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"data": map[string]any{
			"occurrence_id": occurrenceID,
			"sold":          sold,
			"held":          held,
		},
	})
}

func (s *Server) handleGetGaAvailability(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	occurrenceID := r.URL.Query().Get("occurrence_id")
	zoneID := r.URL.Query().Get("ga_zone_id")
	if occurrenceID == "" || zoneID == "" {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "missing_params", "")
		return
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	resp, err := s.handler.GetGaAvailability(ctx, &inventoryv1.GetGaAvailabilityRequest{
		EventOccurrenceId: occurrenceID,
		GaZoneId:          zoneID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "grpc_error", err.Error())
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{
		"data": map[string]any{
			"total_capacity": resp.TotalCapacity,
			"held":           resp.Held,
			"sold":           resp.Sold,
			"available":      resp.Available,
		},
	})
}

// ─── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, body any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(body)
}

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string) {
	writeJSON(w, status, map[string]any{
		"error":   code,
		"message": message,
	})
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RequestContext — carried by every request for tracing
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
type RequestContext struct {
	state         protoimpl.MessageState `protogen:"open.v1"`
	RequestId     string                 `protobuf:"bytes,1,opt,name=request_id,json=requestId,proto3" json:"request_id,omitempty"`       // UUID v4 for distributed tracing
	UserId        string                 `protobuf:"bytes,2,opt,name=user_id,json=userId,proto3" json:"user_id,omitempty"`                // ULID of the acting user (empty if guest)
	OrganizerId   string                 `protobuf:"bytes,3,opt,name=organizer_id,json=organizerId,proto3" json:"organizer_id,omitempty"` // ULID of the organizer context (empty if buyer)
	IpAddress     string                 `protobuf:"bytes,4,opt,name=ip_address,json=ipAddress,proto3" json:"ip_address,omitempty"`       // Client IP, for audit and anti-fraud
	UserAgent     string                 `protobuf:"bytes,5,opt,name=user_agent,json=userAgent,proto3" json:"user_agent,omitempty"`       // Truncated to 500 chars
	RequestedAt   *timestamppb.Timestamp `protobuf:"bytes,6,opt,name=requested_at,json=requestedAt,proto3" json:"requested_at,omitempty"`
	unknownFields protoimpl.UnknownFields
	sizeCache     protoimpl.SizeCache
